# Plan: Migrate BOS-ARSA to Eleventy (11ty)

## Context

The BOS-ARSA website has 4 HTML pages with massive code duplication — nav, footer, CSS variables, font-face declarations, favicons, and JS are copy-pasted across every file. Adding a new page means copying 150+ lines of boilerplate. The press page has 6 hand-coded article cards that will grow over time. SEO files (robots.txt, sitemap.xml, llms.txt) are also manually maintained.

**Goal:** Migrate to Eleventy (11ty) — a zero-config static site generator that outputs plain HTML. The nginx/Docker deployment stays identical, we just add a build step.

## Why Eleventy

- Zero-config, minimal dependencies (~1 npm package)
- Nunjucks templating: layouts, partials, loops, data files
- Output is plain static HTML — served by nginx exactly as before
- JSON data files for press articles (add article = add JSON entry)
- Can generate sitemap.xml, robots.txt, llms.txt from templates
- No client-side framework — same HTML output as today

## New Project Structure

```
bosarsa/
├── src/
│   ├── _includes/
│   │   ├── base.njk              # Full HTML document shell
│   │   ├── nav.njk               # Full navigation (logo, links, hamburger)
│   │   └── footer.njk            # Full 3-column footer
│   ├── _data/
│   │   ├── site.json             # Global: name, url, email, address, ZVR, etc.
│   │   └── presse.json           # Press articles array
│   ├── css/
│   │   └── base.css              # All shared CSS (vars, fonts, nav, footer, responsive)
│   ├── index.njk                 # Homepage
│   ├── presse.njk                # Press page (loops over presse.json)
│   ├── impressum.njk             # Impressum (content only, layout from base)
│   ├── datenschutz.njk           # Datenschutz (content only, layout from base)
│   ├── robots.njk                # -> robots.txt
│   ├── sitemap.njk               # -> sitemap.xml (auto-lists all pages)
│   └── llms.njk                  # -> llms.txt (pulls from site.json)
├── fonts/                         # Stays in root (passthrough copy)
├── favicon/                       # Stays in root (passthrough copy)
├── *.mp4, *.png, *.webp          # Static assets (passthrough copy)
├── .eleventy.js                   # Eleventy config
├── package.json                   # Just @11ty/eleventy
├── Dockerfile                     # Multi-stage: node build -> nginx serve
├── docker-compose.yml             # Unchanged
├── deploy-production.sh           # Unchanged
└── nginx.conf                     # Unchanged
```

## Key Files

### 1. `src/_data/site.json` — Single source of truth
```json
{
  "name": "BOS-ARSA",
  "fullName": "Österreichischer Amateurfunkverein der Behörden und Organisationen mit Sicherheitsaufgaben",
  "legalName": "BOS-Amateur-Radio-Society-Austria (BOS-ARSA)",
  "url": "https://bosarsa.oeradio.at",
  "email": "office@bos-arsa.at",
  "address": { ... },
  "zvr": "1783847541",
  "vereinsbehoerde": "Landespolizeidirektion Kärnten"
}
```

### 2. `src/_data/presse.json` — Data-driven press articles
```json
[
  {
    "date": "22. April 2022",
    "tag": "blog",
    "tagLabel": "Blog",
    "title": "Not- und Katastrophenfunk — ...",
    "url": "https://www.mh-is.at/?p=1158",
    "source": "mh-is.at — OE3EGH und OE3XEG",
    "desc": "Ausführlicher Überblick..."
  }
]
```

### 3. `src/_includes/base.njk` — Single layout for all pages
- `<head>` with fonts, CSS, favicons, SEO meta (title, description, canonical, OG, Twitter)
- Includes `nav.njk` and `footer.njk`
- Content block for page-specific content
- Shared JS at bottom (lucide, year, hamburger, scroll)
- Front matter controls: `title`, `description`, `canonical`, `activeNav`, `pageCSS`

### 4. `src/presse.njk` — Loop replaces 6 copy-pasted cards
```nunjucks
{% for article in presse %}
  <a class="article" href="{{ article.url }}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
    <div class="article__date">{{ article.date }}
      <span class="article__tag article__tag--{{ article.tag }}">{{ article.tagLabel }}</span>
    </div>
    <h2 class="article__title">{{ article.title }}</h2>
    <div class="article__source">{{ article.source }}</div>
    <p class="article__desc">{{ article.desc }}</p>
  </a>
{% endfor %}
```

### 5. SEO files — Generated from data
- **sitemap.njk**: Loops over 11ty's `collections.all` to auto-list all pages
- **robots.njk**: Simple template referencing `site.url`
- **llms.njk**: Pulls org info from `site.json`, board members, etc.

### 6. `Dockerfile` — Multi-stage build
```dockerfile
FROM node:20-alpine AS build
WORKDIR /app
COPY package*.json ./
RUN npm ci --production
COPY . .
RUN npx @11ty/eleventy

FROM nginx:alpine
RUN rm /etc/nginx/conf.d/default.conf
COPY nginx.conf /etc/nginx/conf.d/bosarsa.conf
COPY --from=build /app/_site/ /usr/share/nginx/html/
EXPOSE 80
```

### 7. `.eleventy.js` — Config
- Input: `src/`, Output: `_site/`
- Passthrough copy: `fonts/`, `favicon/`, `*.mp4`, `*.png`, `*.webp`, `nginx.conf`
- Nunjucks as template engine

## Also update impressum & datenschutz

Both currently have the old simple nav/footer. They'll get the full nav and full footer from the shared includes — consistent with presse.html and index.html.

## What stays the same

- `nginx.conf` — identical
- `docker-compose.yml` — identical
- `deploy-production.sh` — identical
- All static assets (fonts, favicons, videos, images)
- The final HTML output looks exactly the same to visitors

## Implementation Steps

1. `npm init` + install `@11ty/eleventy`
2. Create `.eleventy.js` config
3. Create `src/_data/site.json` and `src/_data/presse.json`
4. Create `src/css/base.css` with all shared styles
5. Create `src/_includes/base.njk`, `nav.njk`, `footer.njk`
6. Convert `index.html` -> `src/index.njk`
7. Convert `presse.html` -> `src/presse.njk` (data-driven loop)
8. Convert `impressum.html` -> `src/impressum.njk`
9. Convert `datenschutz.html` -> `src/datenschutz.njk`
10. Create `src/robots.njk`, `src/sitemap.njk`, `src/llms.njk`
11. Update `Dockerfile` to multi-stage build
12. Update `.gitignore` (add `_site/`, `node_modules/`)
13. Delete old root HTML files
14. Test build locally, verify output matches current site
15. Deploy

## Verification

1. Run `npx @11ty/eleventy` — should produce `_site/` with all files
2. Compare `_site/index.html` output with current `index.html`
3. Verify `_site/presse.html` renders all 6 articles
4. Verify `_site/sitemap.xml`, `_site/robots.txt`, `_site/llms.txt` content
5. Deploy and verify live site at https://bosarsa.oeradio.at

   264→      <div class="article__date">10. Dezember 2021 <span class="article__tag article__tag--podcast">Podcast</span></div>
   265→      <h2 class="article__title">BOS-ARSA, Ausbildung</h2>
   266→      <div class="article__source">ON AIR — Amateurfunk D-A-CH Podcast, Staffel 2, Folge 1 — Host: Michael Wurzinger (OE3CMW), Gast: Lukas Spitzer (OE8LSR)</div>
   267→      <p class="article__desc">Lukas Spitzer berichtet über Aus- und Weiterbildung innerhalb der BOS-ARSA Gruppe und die Schnittstellen zwischen dem ÖVSV-Notfunksystem und den Leitstellen der Sicherheitsorganisationen.</p>
   268→    </a>
   269→
   270→    <a class="article" href="https://podcasts.apple.com/at/podcast/bos-arsa-notfunkbr%C3%REDACTED?i=1000543228793" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
   271→      <div class="article__date">27. November 2021 <span class="article__tag article__tag--podcast">Podcast</span></div>
   272→      <h2 class="article__title">BOS-ARSA, Notfunkbrücke zu den Blaulichtorganisationen</h2>
   273→      <div class="article__source">ON AIR — Amateurfunk D-A-CH Podcast, Staffel 1, Folge 12 — Host: Michael Wurzinger (OE3CMW), Gast: Wolfgang Gabrutsch (OE8GWQ)</div>
   274→      <p class="article__desc">BOS-ARSA Obmann Wolfgang Gabrutsch stellt das Notfunkprojekt vor — eine Brücke zwischen dem Amateurfunk und den Funkdiensten der Blaulichtorganisationen für den Krisenfall.</p>
   275→    </a>
   276→
   277→    <a class="article" href="https://www.oewr-kaernten.at/cms/blog/amateurfunkkurs-4963/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
   278→      <div class="article__date">September 2021 <span class="article__tag article__tag--kurs">Ausbildung</span></div>
   279→      <h2 class="article__title">Amateurfunkkurs — 8 Einsatzkräfte erfolgreich lizenziert</h2>
   280→      <div class="article__source">Österreichische Wasserrettung Kärnten</div>
   281→      <p class="article__desc">BOS-ARSA organisierte einen fünftägigen Amateurfunkkurs bei der Rot-Kreuz-Rettungsstelle in Ferlach. Acht Einsatzkräfte der Wasserrettung bestanden die Prüfung und erhielten die CEPT-Lizenz. Die neuen Operatorinnen und Operatoren unterstützen künftig den Katastrophenschutz.</p>
   282→    </a>
   283→
   284→    <a class="article" href="https://www.oevsv.REDACTED" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
   285→      <div class="article__date">13. Juli 2021 <span class="article__tag article__tag--artikel">Artikel</span></div>
   286→      <h2 class="article__title">BOS-ARSA und NOTFUNK — Fachgespräch in Kärnten</h2>
   287→      <div class="article__source">ÖVSV — Österreichischer Versuchssenderverband — Kevin Mekul (OE8KKM)</div>
   288→      <p class="article__desc">Bericht über ein Fachgespräch zum Thema Notfunk am Pressegger See. Beim Blaulicht-Stammtisch in Bleiburg wurde die Einrichtung einer Funkstelle im Krisenmanagementzentrum der Bezirkshauptmannschaft Völkermarkt besprochen.</p>
   289→    </a>
   290→
   291→    <a class="article" href="https://www.meinbezirk.at/klagenfurt-land/c-leute/die-welt-der-funkamateure_a4596338" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
   292→      <div class="article__date">28. April 2021 <span class="article__tag article__tag--artikel">Artikel</span></div>
   293→      <h2 class="article__title">Kurzwelle &amp; Co: Die Welt der Funkamateure</h2>
   294→      <div class="article__source">MeinBezirk.at — Klagenfurt Land — Bernhard Knaus</div>
   295→      <p class="article__desc">Regionaler Zeitungsartikel über die Welt des Amateurfunks mit Oberst Wolfgang Gabrutsch im Mittelpunkt. Berichtet über die Gründung von BOS-ARSA 2019, die rund 80 Mitglieder und den Aufbau von Relaisfunkstellen mit autonomer Stromversorgung für die Krisenkommunikation.</p>
   296→    </a>

   352→      nav.classList.toggle('scrolled', window.scrollY > 20);
   353→    }, { passive: true });
   354→
   355→    // Mobile menu
   356→    const navToggle = document.getElementById('navToggle');
   357→    const navLinks = document.getElementById('navLinks');
   358→    navToggle.addEventListener('click', () => {
   359→      const isOpen = navLinks.classList.toggle('open');
   360→      navToggle.classList.toggle('active');
   361→      navToggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', isOpen);
   362→      navToggle.setAttribute('aria-label', isOpen ? 'Menü schließen' : 'Menü öffnen');
   363→      document.body.style.overflow = isOpen ? 'hidden' : '';
   364→    });
   365→    navLinks.querySelectorAll('a').forEach(link => {
   366→      link.addEventListener('click', () => {
   367→        navLinks.classList.remove('open');
   368→        navToggle.classList.remove('active');
   369→        navToggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
   370→        document.body.style.overflow = '';
   371→      });
   372→    });
   373→  </script>

   101→    *, *::before, *::after { margin: 0; padding: 0; box-sizing: border-box; }
   102→
   103→    :root {
   104→      /* Austrian Police Uniform Blues — derived from Bundespolizei dunkelblau */
   105→      --polizei-dark:    #0F1A2E;   /* Deepest navy — near-black uniform shadow */
   106→      --polizei-navy:    #1B2A4A;   /* Standard Austrian police uniform dark blue */
   107→      --polizei-blue:    #2B4370;   /* Lighter uniform blue — daylight appearance */
   108→      --polizei-mid:     #3A5A8C;   /* Mid-blue for interactive elements */
   109→      --polizei-light:   #4A7AB5;   /* Bright blue accent — hover / links */
   110→      --polizei-pale:    #6B9DD6;   /* Pale blue for soft highlights */
   111→
   112→      /* Austrian flag red + BOS-ARSA logo gold */
   113→      --at-red:          #C8102E;   /* Austrian Bundesfarbe Rot */
   114→      --bos-gold:        #D4A843;   /* Gold from BOS-ARSA logo lettering */
   115→      --bos-gold-light:  #E0BE6A;   /* Lighter gold for hover */
   116→      --bos-gold-dark:   #B8912E;   /* Darker gold for active */
   117→
   118→      /* Neutrals */
   119→      --white:           #FFFFFF;
   120→      --off-white:       #F4F6F9;
   121→      --gray-100:        #E8ECF1;
   122→      --gray-200:        #D0D7E0;
   123→      --gray-300:        #A8B4C2;
   124→      --gray-500:        #5C6B7F;
   125→      --gray-700:        #374151;
   126→      --gray-900:        #111827;
   127→
   128→      --font-main: 'Inter', -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', sans-serif;
   129→      --font-mono: 'JetBrains Mono', monospace;
   130→      --container: 1140px;
   131→      --nav-h: 72px;
   132→      --radius: 2px;
   133→    }
   134→
   135→    html { scroll-behavior: smooth; scroll-padding-top: var(--nav-h); }
   136→
   137→    body {
   138→      font-family: var(--font-main);
   139→      color: var(--gray-700);
   140→      background: var(--off-white);
   141→      line-height: 1.65;
   142→      -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
   143→    }
   144→
   145→    img { max-width: 100%; display: block; }
   146→    a { color: var(--polizei-light); text-decoration: none; transition: color .2s; }
   147→    a:hover { color: var(--polizei-pale); }
   148→    a:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid var(--polizei-light); outline-offset: 2px; }
   149→
   150→    .container { max-width: var(--container); margin: 0 auto; padding: 0 24px; }
   151→
   152→    /* ===== Scroll Reveal ===== */
   153→    .reveal {
   154→      opacity: 0;
   155→      transform: translateY(24px);
   156→      transition: opacity .6s ease, transform .6s ease;
   157→    }
   158→    .reveal.visible { opacity: 1; transform: none; }
   159→
   160→    /* ===== Nav ===== */
   161→    .nav {
   162→      position: fixed; top: 0; left: 0; right: 0; z-index: 1000;
   163→      background: var(--polizei-dark);
   164→      height: var(--nav-h);
   165→      transition: box-shadow .3s, background .3s;
   166→      border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.04);
   167→    }
   168→    .nav.scrolled { box-shadow: 0 4px 24px rgba(0,0,0,.35); }
   169→    .nav__inner { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; height: 100%; }
   170→
   171→    .nav__brand {
   172→      display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 12px;
   173→      text-decoration: none; color: var(--white);
   174→    }
   175→    .nav__brand:hover { color: var(--white); }
   176→    .nav__brand-logo {
   177→      height: 44px;
   178→      width: auto;
   179→      border-radius: var(--radius);
   180→    }
   181→    .nav__brand-text {
   182→      display: flex; flex-direction: column; line-height: 1.15;
   183→    }
   184→    .nav__brand-name {
   185→      font-weight: 800; font-size: 1.25rem; letter-spacing: .5px;
   186→      color: var(--bos-gold);
   187→    }
   188→    .nav__brand-sub {
   189→      font-size: .65rem; font-weight: 400; letter-spacing: .3px;
   190→      color: var(--gray-300); white-space: nowrap;
   191→    }
   192→
   193→    .nav__links { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 4px; list-style: none; }
   194→    .nav__links a {
   195→      display: block; padding: 8px 16px;
   196→      color: var(--gray-300); font-size: .875rem; font-weight: 500;
   197→      letter-spacing: .2px; border-radius: var(--radius);
   198→      transition: color .2s, background .2s; text-decoration: none;
   199→    }
   200→    .nav__links a:hover { color: var(--white); background: rgba(255,255,255,.06); }
   201→
   202→    .nav__hamburger {
   203→      display: none; flex-direction: column; justify-content: center;
   204→      gap: 5px; width: 40px; height: 40px;
   205→      background: none; border: none; cursor: pointer; padding: 6px;
   206→    }
   207→    .nav__hamburger span {
   208→      display: block; width: 100%; height: 2px;
   209→      background: var(--gray-300); transition: transform .3s, opacity .3s;
   210→      border-radius: 1px;
   211→    }
   212→    .nav__hamburger.active span:nth-child(1) { transform: translateY(7px) rotate(45deg); }
   213→    .nav__hamburger.active span:nth-child(2) { opacity: 0; }
   214→    .nav__hamburger.active span:nth-child(3) { transform: translateY(-7px) rotate(-45deg); }
   215→
   216→    /* ===== Hero ===== */
   217→    .hero {
   218→      position: relative;
   219→      min-height: 100vh;
   220→      display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;
   221→      background: var(--polizei-dark);
   222→      overflow: hidden;
   223→      padding: calc(var(--nav-h) + 48px) 24px 80px;
   224→    }
   225→
   226→    /* Gradient overlay for depth */
   227→    .hero::before {
   228→      content: '';
   229→      position: absolute; inset: 0;
   230→      background: radial-gradient(ellipse at 30% 50%, rgba(43,67,112,.4) 0%, transparent 60%),
   231→                  radial-gradient(ellipse at 70% 80%, rgba(27,42,74,.6) 0%, transparent 50%);
   232→      z-index: 1;
   233→    }
   234→
   235→    /* Subtle grid lines */
   236→    .hero__grid {
   237→      position: absolute; inset: 0;
   238→      background-image:
   239→        linear-gradient(rgba(255,255,255,.025) 1px, transparent 1px),
   240→        linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(255,255,255,.025) 1px, transparent 1px);
   241→      background-size: 80px 80px;
   242→      z-index: 1;
   243→    }
   244→
   245→    /* Radio waves — concentric circles pulsing outward */
   246→    .hero__waves {
   247→      position: absolute; top: 50%; left: 50%;
   248→      transform: translate(-50%, -50%);
   249→      z-index: 1; pointer-events: none;
   250→    }
   251→    .hero__wave {
   252→      position: absolute; top: 50%; left: 50%;
   253→      width: 160px; height: 160px;
   254→      margin: -80px 0 0 -80px;
   255→      border: 1.5px solid rgba(212,168,67,.1);
   256→      border-radius: 50%;
   257→      animation: pulse 5s ease-out infinite;
   258→    }
   259→    .hero__wave:nth-child(2) { animation-delay: 1.25s; }
   260→    .hero__wave:nth-child(3) { animation-delay: 2.5s; }
   261→    .hero__wave:nth-child(4) { animation-delay: 3.75s; }
   262→    @keyframes pulse {
   263→      0% { width: 160px; height: 160px; margin: -80px 0 0 -80px; opacity: .45; }
   264→      100% { width: 1400px; height: 1400px; margin: -700px 0 0 -700px; opacity: 0; }
   265→    }
   266→
   267→    .hero__content {
   268→      position: relative; z-index: 2;
   269→      text-align: center; max-width: 780px;
   270→    }
   271→
   272→    .hero__logo {
   273→      width: 420px; max-width: 80vw;
   274→      height: auto;
   275→      margin: 0 auto 36px;
   276→      border-radius: 4px;
   277→      box-shadow: 0 8px 40px rgba(0,0,0,.3);
   278→    }
   279→
   280→    .hero__title {
   281→      font-size: clamp(1.8rem, 4.5vw, 3rem);
   282→      font-weight: 800; color: var(--white);
   283→      line-height: 1.15; letter-spacing: -.3px;
   284→      margin-bottom: 16px;
   285→    }
   286→    .hero__title span { color: var(--bos-gold); }
   287→
   288→    .hero__subtitle {
   289→      font-size: clamp(.95rem, 1.8vw, 1.15rem);
   290→      color: var(--gray-300); font-weight: 400;
   291→      line-height: 1.65; max-width: 600px; margin: 0 auto 40px;
   292→    }
   293→
   294→    .hero__actions { display: flex; gap: 14px; justify-content: center; flex-wrap: wrap; }
   295→
   296→    /* ===== Buttons ===== */
   297→    .btn {
   298→      display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px;
   299→      padding: 13px 28px; font-family: var(--font-main);
   300→      font-size: .875rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: .3px;
   301→      border: 2px solid transparent; border-radius: var(--radius);
   302→      cursor: pointer; transition: all .2s; text-decoration: none;
   303→    }
   304→    .btn:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid var(--polizei-light); outline-offset: 2px; }
   305→
   306→    .btn--gold {
   307→      background: var(--bos-gold); color: var(--polizei-dark);
   308→      border-color: var(--bos-gold);
   309→    }
   310→    .btn--gold:hover {
   311→      background: var(--bos-gold-light); border-color: var(--bos-gold-light);
   312→      color: var(--polizei-dark); transform: translateY(-1px);
   313→      box-shadow: 0 4px 16px rgba(212,168,67,.25);
   314→    }
   315→
   316→    .btn--outline-white {
   317→      background: transparent; color: var(--white);
   318→      border-color: rgba(255,255,255,.25);
   319→    }
   320→    .btn--outline-white:hover {
   321→      border-color: var(--white); color: var(--white);
   322→      background: rgba(255,255,255,.05); transform: translateY(-1px);
   323→    }
   324→
   325→    .btn--polizei {
   326→      background: var(--polizei-mid); color: var(--white);
   327→      border-color: var(--polizei-mid);
   328→    }
   329→    .btn--polizei:hover {
   330→      background: var(--polizei-light); border-color: var(--polizei-light);
   331→      color: var(--white); transform: translateY(-1px);
   332→      box-shadow: 0 4px 16px rgba(74,122,181,.25);
   333→    }
   334→
   335→    /* ===== Austrian flag stripe accent ===== */
   336→    .at-stripe {
   337→      height: 4px; width: 100%;
   338→      background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--at-red) 33.33%, var(--white) 33.33%, var(--white) 66.66%, var(--at-red) 66.66%);
   339→    }
   340→
   341→    /* ===== Sections ===== */
   342→    .section { padding: 96px 0; }
   343→    .section--alt { background: var(--white); }
   344→    .section--dark {
   345→      background: linear-gradient(170deg, var(--polizei-navy) 0%, var(--polizei-dark) 100%);
   346→      color: var(--white);
   347→    }
   348→
   349→    .section__header { text-align: center; margin-bottom: 56px; }
   350→    .section__label {
   351→      display: inline-block;
   352→      font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: .72rem;
   353→      letter-spacing: 3px; text-transform: uppercase;
   354→      color: var(--bos-gold); margin-bottom: 10px;
   355→    }
   356→    .section__title {
   357→      font-size: clamp(1.6rem, 3.5vw, 2.2rem);
   358→      font-weight: 800; line-height: 1.2; color: var(--gray-900);
   359→    }
   360→    .section--dark .section__title { color: var(--white); }
   361→    .section__rule {
   362→      width: 48px; height: 3px; background: var(--bos-gold);
   363→      margin: 14px auto 0; border-radius: 1px;
   364→    }
   365→    .section__intro {
   366→      max-width: 640px; margin: 20px auto 0;
   367→      color: var(--gray-500); font-size: 1rem; line-height: 1.7; text-align: center;
   368→    }
   369→    .section--dark .section__intro { color: var(--gray-300); }
   370→
   371→    /* ===== Video ===== */
   372→    .video-wrapper {
   373→      max-width: 860px;
   374→      margin: 0 auto;
   375→      border-radius: var(--radius);
   376→      overflow: hidden;
   377→      box-shadow: 0 8px 32px rgba(15,26,46,.12);
   378→      border: 1px solid var(--gray-100);
   379→      background: var(--polizei-dark);
   380→    }
   381→    .video-player {
   382→      width: 100%;
   383→      display: block;
   384→    }
   385→
   386→    /* ===== Feature Cards (Über uns) ===== */
   387→    .features { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(3,1fr); gap: 24px; }
   388→    .feature-card {
   389→      background: var(--white); border: 1px solid var(--gray-100);
   390→      border-radius: var(--radius); padding: 36px 28px;
   391→      transition: transform .25s, box-shadow .25s;
   392→      position: relative;
   393→    }
   394→    .feature-card::after {
   395→      content: ''; position: absolute; bottom: 0; left: 0; right: 0;
   396→      height: 3px; background: var(--polizei-mid);
   397→      transform: scaleX(0); transform-origin: left; transition: transform .35s;
   398→    }
   399→    .feature-card:hover {
   400→      transform: translateY(-3px);
   401→      box-shadow: 0 8px 28px rgba(15,26,46,.08);
   402→    }
   403→    .feature-card:hover::after { transform: scaleX(1); }
   404→
   405→    .feature-card__icon {
   406→      width: 48px; height: 48px;
   407→      display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;
   408→      background: var(--polizei-navy); color: var(--bos-gold);
   409→      border-radius: var(--radius); margin-bottom: 18px;
   410→    }
   411→    .feature-card__title {
   412→      font-size: 1.05rem; font-weight: 700;
   413→      color: var(--gray-900); margin-bottom: 8px;
   414→    }
   415→    .feature-card__text {
   416→      color: var(--gray-500); font-size: .9rem; line-height: 1.6;
   417→    }
   418→
   419→    /* ===== Board Grid ===== */
   420→    .board-grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(3,1fr); gap: 20px; }
   421→    .board-card {
   422→      background: var(--off-white); border: 1px solid var(--gray-100);
   423→      border-radius: var(--radius); padding: 32px 24px; text-align: center;
   424→      transition: transform .25s, box-shadow .25s;
   425→    }
   426→    .board-card:hover {
   427→      transform: translateY(-2px);
   428→      box-shadow: 0 6px 20px rgba(15,26,46,.06);
   429→    }
   430→    .board-card--president {
   431→      background: var(--polizei-dark);
   432→      border-color: rgba(212,168,67,.2);
   433→    }
   434→    .board-card--president .board-card__name { color: var(--white); }
   435→    .board-card--president .board-card__credentials { color: var(--gray-300); }
   436→
   437→    .board-card__avatar {
   438→      width: 56px; height: 56px; margin: 0 auto 16px;
   439→      display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;
   440→      background: var(--polizei-navy); color: var(--gray-300);
   441→      border-radius: 50%;
   442→    }
   443→    .board-card--president .board-card__avatar {
   444→      background: var(--bos-gold); color: var(--polizei-dark);
   445→    }
   446→
   447→    .board-card__role {
   448→      font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: .68rem;
   449→      letter-spacing: 1.5px; text-transform: uppercase;
   450→      color: var(--bos-gold); margin-bottom: 8px; font-weight: 500;
   451→    }
   452→    .board-card__name {
   453→      font-size: 1.05rem; font-weight: 700; color: var(--gray-900); line-height: 1.25;
   454→    }
   455→    .board-card__credentials {
   456→      font-size: .78rem; color: var(--gray-300); margin-top: 4px;
   457→    }
   458→
   459→    /* ===== Goals ===== */
   460→    .goals-content {
   461→      display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
   462→      gap: 56px; align-items: start;
   463→    }
   464→    .goals__statement {
   465→      font-size: clamp(1.25rem, 2.5vw, 1.65rem);
   466→      font-weight: 700; color: var(--white); line-height: 1.45;
   467→    }
   468→    .goals__statement span { color: var(--bos-gold); }
   469→
   470→    .goals-list { list-style: none; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 14px; }
   471→    .goals-list__item {
   472→      display: flex; align-items: flex-start; gap: 14px;
   473→      padding: 16px 18px;
   474→      background: rgba(255,255,255,.04);
   475→      border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.06);
   476→      border-radius: var(--radius);
   477→      transition: background .2s;
   478→    }
   479→    .goals-list__item:hover { background: rgba(255,255,255,.07); }
   480→    .goals-list__icon {
   481→      flex-shrink: 0; width: 36px; height: 36px;
   482→      display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;
   483→      background: rgba(212,168,67,.1); border-radius: var(--radius);
   484→      color: var(--bos-gold);
   485→    }
   486→    .goals-list__text {
   487→      color: var(--gray-300); font-size: .92rem; line-height: 1.5; padding-top: 6px;
   488→    }
   489→
   490→    /* ===== Join Section ===== */
   491→    .join { position: relative; overflow: hidden; }
   492→    .join__title {
   493→      font-size: clamp(1.5rem, 3vw, 2rem);
   494→      font-weight: 800; color: var(--gray-900);
   495→      margin-bottom: 14px; line-height: 1.2; text-align: center;
   496→    }
   497→    .join__subtitle {
   498→      color: var(--gray-500); font-size: 1rem; line-height: 1.7; margin-bottom: 40px; text-align: center;
   499→      max-width: 680px; margin-left: auto; margin-right: auto;
   500→    }
   501→    .join__orgs {
   502→      display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr);
   503→      gap: 16px; margin-bottom: 48px;
   504→    }
   505→    .join__org {
   506→      display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; gap: 10px;
   507→      padding: 20px 12px; background: var(--white); border-radius: var(--radius);
   508→      border: 1px solid var(--gray-100); text-decoration: none;
   509→      transition: all .25s ease;
   510→    }
   511→    .join__org:hover {
   512→      border-color: var(--polizei-mid); box-shadow: 0 4px 16px rgba(59,90,140,.12);
   513→      transform: translateY(-2px);
   514→    }
   515→    .join__org-icon {
   516→      width: 44px; height: 44px; border-radius: 50%;
   517→      background: rgba(27,42,74,.08);
   518→      display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;
   519→      flex-shrink: 0;
   520→    }
   521→    .join__org-icon i { width: 22px; height: 22px; color: var(--polizei-mid); }
   522→    .join__org:hover .join__org-icon { background: var(--polizei-mid); }
   523→    .join__org:hover .join__org-icon i { color: var(--white); }
   524→    .join__org-name {
   525→      font-size: .8rem; font-weight: 600; color: var(--gray-700);
   526→      text-align: center; line-height: 1.3;
   527→    }
   528→
   529→    .join__divider {
   530→      display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 16px; margin-bottom: 40px;
   531→    }
   532→    .join__divider::before, .join__divider::after {
   533→      content: ''; flex: 1; height: 1px; background: var(--gray-100);
   534→    }
   535→    .join__divider-text {
   536→      font-size: .78rem; font-weight: 600; color: var(--gray-300);
   537→      text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 1.5px; white-space: nowrap;
   538→    }
   539→
   540→    .join__foerder {
   541→      background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--polizei-dark), var(--polizei-navy));
   542→      border-radius: var(--radius); padding: 36px 32px;
   543→      display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 24px;
   544→      position: relative; overflow: hidden;
   545→    }
   546→    .join__foerder::before {
   547→      content: ''; position: absolute; inset: 0; opacity: .03;
   548→      background-image:
   549→        linear-gradient(rgba(255,255,255,.1) 1px, transparent 1px),
   550→        linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(255,255,255,.1) 1px, transparent 1px);
   551→      background-size: 40px 40px;
   552→    }
   553→    .join__foerder-icon {
   554→      width: 56px; height: 56px; border-radius: 50%;
   555→      background: rgba(212,168,67,.15); display: flex;
   556→      align-items: center; justify-content: center; flex-shrink: 0;
   557→      position: relative; z-index: 1;
   558→    }
   559→    .join__foerder-icon i { width: 26px; height: 26px; color: var(--bos-gold); }
   560→    .join__foerder-content { position: relative; z-index: 1; flex: 1; }
   561→    .join__foerder-title {
   562→      font-size: 1.05rem; font-weight: 700; color: var(--white); margin-bottom: 6px;
   563→    }
   564→    .join__foerder-text {
   565→      font-size: .88rem; color: var(--gray-300); line-height: 1.6; margin-bottom: 16px;
   566→    }
   567→    .join__foerder .btn { position: relative; z-index: 1; }
   568→
   569→    .join__cta {
   570→      text-align: center; margin-top: 40px;
   571→    }
   572→
   573→    /* ===== Partners ===== */
   574→    .partners { background: var(--white); border-top: 1px solid var(--gray-100); }
   575→    .partners__label {
   576→      text-align: center; font-size: .85rem;
   577→      color: var(--gray-300); margin-bottom: 28px; letter-spacing: .3px;
   578→    }
   579→    .partners-grid { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; gap: 40px; flex-wrap: wrap; }
   580→    .partner-item {
   581→      display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 14px;
   582→      padding: 20px 28px; border: 1px solid var(--gray-100);
   583→      border-radius: var(--radius); text-decoration: none; color: var(--gray-700);
   584→      transition: border-color .2s, box-shadow .2s;
   585→    }
   586→    .partner-item:hover {
   587→      border-color: var(--polizei-mid);
   588→      box-shadow: 0 4px 12px rgba(15,26,46,.05);
   589→      color: var(--polizei-navy);
   590→    }
   591→    .partner-item__logo { height: 36px; width: auto; }
   592→    .partner-item__info { display: flex; flex-direction: column; }
   593→    .partner-item__name { font-weight: 700; font-size: .95rem; }
   594→    .partner-item__desc { font-size: .78rem; color: var(--gray-300); }
   595→
   596→    /* ===== Footer ===== */
   597→    .footer {
   598→      background: var(--polizei-dark); color: var(--gray-300); padding: 56px 0 0;
   599→    }
   600→    .footer__inner { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 2fr 1fr 1fr; gap: 48px; }
   601→    .footer__brand {
   602→      display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 12px; margin-bottom: 18px;
   603→    }
   604→    .footer__brand-logo { height: 40px; width: auto; border-radius: 2px; }
   605→    .footer__brand-name {
   606→      font-weight: 800; font-size: 1.1rem; color: var(--bos-gold); letter-spacing: .5px;
   607→    }
   608→    .footer__org-name {
   609→      font-size: .84rem; line-height: 1.6;
   610→      color: rgba(168,180,194,.6); margin-bottom: 16px;
   611→    }
   612→    .footer__details {
   613→      font-size: .82rem; line-height: 1.75; color: rgba(168,180,194,.5);
   614→    }
   615→    .footer__details a { color: var(--bos-gold); }
   616→    .footer__details a:hover { color: var(--bos-gold-light); }
   617→
   618→    .footer__links-title {
   619→      font-weight: 700; font-size: .85rem; color: var(--white);
   620→      letter-spacing: .5px; margin-bottom: 16px; text-transform: uppercase;
   621→    }
   622→    .footer__link-list { list-style: none; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 8px; }
   623→    .footer__link-list a {
   624→      color: rgba(168,180,194,.5); font-size: .85rem; transition: color .2s;
   625→    }
   626→    .footer__link-list a:hover { color: var(--white); }
   627→
   628→    .footer__bottom {
   629→      margin-top: 40px; padding: 18px 0;
   630→      border-top: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.05);
   631→      display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between;
   632→      font-size: .78rem; color: rgba(168,180,194,.35);
   633→    }
   634→
   635→    /* ===== Responsive ===== */
   636→    @media (max-width: 1024px) {
   637→      .features { grid-template-columns: repeat(2,1fr); }
   638→      .board-grid { grid-template-columns: repeat(2,1fr); }
   639→      .goals-content { grid-template-columns: 1fr; gap: 36px; }
   640→      .goals__statement { text-align: center; }
   641→      .join__orgs { grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr); }
   642→      .join__foerder { flex-direction: column; text-align: center; }
   643→      .footer__inner { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
   644→    }
   645→
   646→    @media (max-width: 768px) {
   647→      :root { --nav-h: 60px; }
   648→      .nav__links {
   649→        position: fixed; top: var(--nav-h); left: 0; right: 0; bottom: 0;
   650→        background: var(--polizei-dark);
   651→        flex-direction: column; align-items: stretch;
   652→        padding: 16px 24px; gap: 2px;
   653→        transform: translateX(100%);
   654→        transition: transform .3s ease;
   655→        overflow-y: auto;
   656→      }
   657→      .nav__links.open { transform: translateX(0); }
   658→      .nav__links a { font-size: 1rem; padding: 14px 16px; border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.04); }
   659→      .nav__hamburger { display: flex; }
   660→      .features { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
   661→      .board-grid { grid-template-columns: 1fr; max-width: 400px; margin: 0 auto; }
   662→      .section { padding: 72px 0; }
   663→      .section__header { margin-bottom: 40px; }
   664→      .hero { min-height: 92vh; }
   665→      .hero__logo { width: 320px; }
   666→      .hero__actions { flex-direction: column; align-items: center; }
   667→      .btn { width: 100%; max-width: 280px; justify-content: center; }
   668→      .partners-grid { flex-direction: column; gap: 16px; }
   669→      .partner-item { width: 100%; max-width: 320px; justify-content: center; }
   670→      .footer__bottom { flex-direction: column; gap: 6px; text-align: center; }
   671→      .join__orgs { grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr); }
   672→      .join__foerder { padding: 28px 20px; }
   673→      .nav__brand-sub { display: none; }
   674→    }
   675→  </style>
   676→</head>
   677→<body>
   678→
   679→  <!-- Nav -->
   680→  <nav class="nav" id="nav" role="navigation" aria-label="Hauptnavigation">
   681→    <div class="container nav__inner">
   682→      <a href="#" class="nav__brand" aria-label="BOS-ARSA Startseite">
   683→        <img src="/logo.webp?v=2" alt="BOS-ARSA Logo" class="nav__brand-logo" width="176" height="70">
   684→        <span class="nav__brand-text">
   685→          <span class="nav__brand-name">BOS-ARSA</span>
   686→          <span class="nav__brand-sub">Amateur-Radio-Society-Austria</span>
   687→        </span>
   688→      </a>
   689→      <button class="nav__hamburger" id="navToggle" aria-label="Menü öffnen" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="navLinks">
   690→        <span></span><span></span><span></span>
   691→      </button>
   692→      <ul class="nav__links" id="navLinks">
   693→        <li><a href="#ueber-uns">Über uns</a></li>
   694→        <li><a href="#video">Videos</a></li>
   695→        <li><a href="#vorstand">Vorstand</a></li>
   696→        <li><a href="#ziele">Ziele</a></li>
   697→        <li><a href="#mitglied">Mitglied werden</a></li>
   698→        <li><a href="/presse.html">Presse</a></li>
   699→        <li><a href="#kontakt">Kontakt</a></li>
   700→      </ul>
   701→    </div>
   702→  </nav>
   703→
   704→  <!-- Hero -->
   705→  <header class="hero" id="hero">
   706→    <div class="hero__grid" aria-hidden="true"></div>
   707→    <div class="hero__waves" aria-hidden="true">
   708→      <div class="hero__wave"></div>
   709→      <div class="hero__wave"></div>
   710→      <div class="hero__wave"></div>
   711→      <div class="hero__wave"></div>
   712→    </div>
   713→    <div class="hero__content">
   714→      <img src="/logo.webp?v=2" alt="BOS-ARSA — Österreichischer Amateurfunkverein der Behörden und Organisationen mit Sicherheitsaufgaben" class="hero__logo" width="800" height="317">
   715→      <h1 class="hero__title">Amateurfunk im Dienst der <span>Sicherheit</span></h1>
   716→      <p class="hero__subtitle">Der österreichische Amateurfunkverein der Behörden und Organisationen mit Sicherheitsaufgaben</p>
   717→      <div class="hero__actions">
   718→        <a href="#mitglied" class="btn btn--gold">
   719→          <i data-lucide="user-plus" style="width:17px;height:17px;"></i>
   720→          Mitglied werden
   721→        </a>
   722→        <a href="#ueber-uns" class="btn btn--outline-white">
   723→          <i data-lucide="chevron-down" style="width:17px;height:17px;"></i>
   724→          Mehr erfahren
   725→        </a>
   726→      </div>
   727→    </div>
   728→  </header>

   739→        <div class="section__rule"></div>
   740→        <p class="section__intro">Der Verein bezweckt die Erhaltung, Förderung und Verbreitung des Amateurfunkwesens innerhalb des Wirkungsbereiches der Behörden und Organisationen mit Sicherheitsaufgaben (BOS).</p>

   746→          </div>
   747→          <h3 class="feature-card__title">Sicherheit</h3>
   748→          <p class="feature-card__text">Amateurfunk als verlässliche Kommunikation für BOS-Organisationen — auch dann, wenn andere Systeme ausfallen.</p>
   749→        </div>
   750→        <div class="feature-card reveal">

   756→        </div>
   757→        <div class="feature-card reveal">

  1022→  <script>
  1023→    lucide.createIcons();
  1024→    document.getElementById('year').textContent = new Date().getFullYear();
  1025→
  1026→    // Nav scroll shadow
  1027→    const nav = document.getElementById('nav');
  1028→    window.addEventListener('scroll', () => {
  1029→      nav.classList.toggle('scrolled', window.scrollY > 20);
  1030→    }, { passive: true });
  1031→
  1032→    // Mobile menu
  1033→    const navToggle = document.getElementById('navToggle');
  1034→    const navLinks = document.getElementById('navLinks');
  1035→    navToggle.addEventListener('click', () => {
  1036→      const isOpen = navLinks.classList.toggle('open');
  1037→      navToggle.classList.toggle('active');
  1038→      navToggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', isOpen);
  1039→      navToggle.setAttribute('aria-label', isOpen ? 'Menü schließen' : 'Menü öffnen');
  1040→      document.body.style.overflow = isOpen ? 'hidden' : '';
  1041→    });
  1042→    navLinks.querySelectorAll('a').forEach(link => {
  1043→      link.addEventListener('click', () => {
  1044→        navLinks.classList.remove('open');
  1045→        navToggle.classList.remove('active');
  1046→        navToggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
  1047→        document.body.style.overflow = '';
  1048→      });
  1049→    });
  1050→
  1051→    // Scroll reveal
  1052→    const observer = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
  1053→      entries.forEach(e => {
  1054→        if (e.isIntersecting) { e.target.classList.add('visible'); observer.unobserve(e.target); }
  1055→      });
  1056→    }, { threshold: 0.1, rootMargin: '0px 0px -30px 0px' });
  1057→    document.querySelectorAll('.reveal').forEach(el => observer.observe(el));
  1058→
  1059→    // Stagger children
  1060→    document.querySelectorAll('.features, .board-grid').forEach(grid => {
  1061→      grid.querySelectorAll('.reveal').forEach((c, i) => { c.style.transitionDelay = `${i * .08}s`; });
  1062→    });
  1063→  </script>
  1064→</body>
  1065→</html>

     1→# BOS-ARSA — Österreichischer Amateurfunkverein der Behörden und Organisationen mit Sicherheitsaufgaben
     2→
     3→> BOS-Amateur-Radio-Society-Austria (BOS-ARSA) ist ein österreichischer Verein, der den Amateurfunk innerhalb der Behörden und Organisationen mit Sicherheitsaufgaben (BOS) fördert. Der Verein stellt sicher, dass Kommunikation auch dann funktioniert, wenn herkömmliche Infrastruktur ausfällt.
     4→
     5→## Über den Verein
     6→
     7→BOS-ARSA wurde gegründet, um den Amateurfunk als zuverlässige Notfall- und Sicherheitskommunikation in Österreich zu etablieren. Der Verein richtet sich an Mitglieder von Behörden und Organisationen mit Sicherheitsaufgaben (BOS), darunter Polizei, Feuerwehr, Rettungsdienste und Katastrophenschutz.
     8→
     9→## Zweck
    10→
    11→Der Verein bezweckt die Erhaltung, Förderung und Verbreitung des Amateurfunkwesens innerhalb des Wirkungsbereiches der Behörden und Organisationen mit Sicherheitsaufgaben (BOS).
    12→
    13→## Ziele
    14→
    15→- Ausbildung & Lizenzierung: Unterstützung bei der Erlangung von Amateurfunklizenzen für BOS-Angehörige
    16→- Notfallkommunikation: Aufbau redundanter Kommunikationswege für Krisensituationen
    17→- Netzwerkaufbau: Vernetzung von Funkamateurinnen und Funkamateuren im BOS-Bereich in ganz Österreich
    18→- Übungen & Training: Regelmäßige Funkübungen und Weiterbildungen
    19→- Zusammenarbeit: Kooperation mit Behörden, Einsatzorganisationen und verwandten Vereinen
    20→
    21→## Vorstand
    22→
    23→- Obmann: Wolfgang Gabrutsch, OE8WGK
    24→- Obmann-Stellvertreter: Patrick Holzer, OE8HPK
    25→- Kassier: Martin Pernull, OE8MPK
    26→- Kassier-Stellvertreterin: Elena Brandstätter, OE8ELB
    27→- Schriftführer: Michael Linder, OE8YML
    28→- Schriftführer-Stellvertreter: Peter Enengl, OE8PEQ
    29→
    30→## Kontakt
    31→
    32→- E-Mail: office@bos-arsa.at
    33→- Sitz: c/o Wolfgang Gabrutsch, Buchengasse 3, 9020 Klagenfurt am Wörthersee, Österreich
    34→- ZVR-Zahl: 1783847541
    35→
    36→## Links
    37→
    38→- Website: https://bosarsa.oeradio.at
    39→- Domain: https://bosarsa.at (leitet weiter auf bosarsa.oeradio.at)
    40→- BOS-ARSA Log: https://bosarsalog.oeradio.at
    41→- Dachverband ÖVSV: https://oevsv.at
    42→

     5→}

    10→
    11→    root /usr/share/nginx/html;
    12→    index index.html;
    13→
    14→    # HTML — always revalidate, never serve stale
    15→    location ~* \.html$ {
    16→        add_header Cache-Control "no-cache, must-revalidate";
    17→        try_files $uri $uri/ =404;
    18→    }
    19→
    20→    location = / {
    21→        add_header Cache-Control "no-cache, must-revalidate";
    22→        try_files $uri $uri/ =404;
    23→    }
    24→
    25→    # Video — cache longer (1 day)
    26→    location ~* \.mp4$ {
    27→        add_header Cache-Control "public, max-age=86400";
    28→    }
    29→
    30→    # Images, fonts, icons — cache 1 week
    31→    location ~* \.(png|webp|ico|svg|woff2?|ttf)$ {
    32→        add_header Cache-Control "public, max-age=604800";
    33→    }
    34→
    35→    # SEO files — cache 1 day
    36→    location ~* (robots\.txt|sitemap\.xml|llms\.txt)$ {
    37→        add_header Cache-Control "public, max-age=86400";
    38→    }
    39→
    40→    location / {
    41→        try_files $uri $uri/ =404;
    42→    }
    43→}

```
     1→#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2→set -e
     3→
     4→RED='\033[0;31m'
     5→GREEN='\033[0;32m'
     6→YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
     7→NC='\033[0m'
     8→
     9→# Load environment variables
    10→if [ -f ".env.production" ]; then
    11→    source .env.production
    12→else
    13→    echo -e "${RED}Error: .env.production file not found${NC}"
    14→    exit 1
    15→fi
    16→
    17→# Validate required variables
    18→REQUIRED_VARS="DEPLOY_HOST REMOTE_DIR CONTAINER_NAME IMAGE_NAME CONTAINER_PORT SITE_URL"
    19→for var in $REQUIRED_VARS; do
    20→    if [ -z "${!var}" ]; then
    21→        echo -e "${RED}Error: $var is not set in .env.production${NC}"
    22→        exit 1
    23→    fi
    24→done
    25→
    26→# Check for --rebuild flag
    27→REBUILD_FLAG=""
    28→if [ "$1" == "--rebuild" ]; then
    29→    REBUILD_FLAG="--no-cache"
    30→    echo -e "${YELLOW}Rebuild flag set — will build without cache${NC}"
    31→fi
    32→
    33→echo -e "${GREEN}=== Deploying BOS-ARSA to $DEPLOY_HOST ===${NC}"
    34→echo "Host: $DEPLOY_HOST"
    35→echo "Remote dir: $REMOTE_DIR"
    36→echo "Container: $CONTAINER_NAME"
    37→echo "Port: $CONTAINER_PORT"
    38→echo ""
    39→
    40→# Step 1: Ensure remote directory exists and sync files
    41→echo -e "${GREEN}Step 1: Syncing files...${NC}"
    42→ssh "$DEPLOY_HOST" "mkdir -p '$REMOTE_DIR'"
    43→rsync -avz --delete \
    44→    --exclude '.git' \
    45→    --exclude '.env.production' \
    46→    ./ "$DEPLOY_HOST:$REMOTE_DIR/"
    47→
    48→# Step 2: Build and start container
    49→echo -e "${GREEN}Step 2: Building and starting container...${NC}"
    50→ssh "$DEPLOY_HOST" "
    51→    cd '$REMOTE_DIR'
    52→    docker build $REBUILD_FLAG -t '$IMAGE_NAME' .
    53→    docker compose up -d --force-recreate --build
    54→"
    55→
    56→# Step 3: Verify deployment
    57→echo -e "${GREEN}Step 3: Verifying deployment...${NC}"
    58→sleep 3
    59→
    60→CONTAINER_STATUS=$(ssh "$DEPLOY_HOST" "docker ps --filter 'name=$CONTAINER_NAME' --format '{{.Status}}'")
    61→echo "Container status: $CONTAINER_STATUS"
    62→
    63→LOCAL_PORT=$(echo $CONTAINER_PORT | cut -d: -f1)
    64→if ssh "$DEPLOY_HOST" "curl -sf -o /dev/null http://localhost:$LOCAL_PORT"; then
    65→    echo -e "${GREEN}✓ Container is running on port $LOCAL_PORT${NC}"
    66→else
    67→    echo -e "${YELLOW}⚠ Container not yet responding on port $LOCAL_PORT${NC}"
    68→fi
    69→
    70→if curl -sf -o /dev/null "$SITE_URL" 2>/dev/null; then
    71→    echo -e "${GREEN}✓ Site is accessible at $SITE_URL${NC}"
    72→else
    73→    echo -e "${YELLOW}⚠ Public URL not yet accessible — configure Cloudflare tunnel${NC}"
    74→fi
    75→
    76→echo ""
    77→echo -e "${GREEN}=== Deployment complete ===${NC}"
    78→

}\n.nav__inner { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; height: 100%; }\n\n.nav__brand {\n  display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 12px;\n  text-decoration: none; color: var(--white);\n}\n.nav__brand:hover { color: var(--white); }\n.nav__brand-logo {\n  height: 44px;\n  width: auto;\n  border-radius: var(--radius);\n}\n.nav__brand-text {\n  display: flex; flex-direction: column; line-height: 1.15;\n}\n.nav__brand-name {\n  font-weight: 800; font-size: 1.25rem; letter-spacing: .5px;\n  color: var(--bos-gold);\n}\n.nav__brand-sub {\n  font-size: .65rem; font-weight: 400; letter-spacing: .3px;\n  color: var(--gray-300); white-space: nowrap;\n}\n\n.nav__links { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 4px; list-style: none; }\n.nav__links a {\n  display: block; padding: 8px 16px;\n  color: var(--gray-300); font-size: .875rem; font-weight: 500;\n  letter-spacing: .2px; border-radius: var(--radius);\n  transition: color .2s, background .2s; text-decoration: none;\n}\n.nav__links a:hover { color: var(--white); background: rgba(255,255,255,.06); }\n.nav__links a.active { color: var(--bos-gold); }\n\n.nav__hamburger {\n  display: none; flex-direction: column; justify-content: center;\n  gap: 5px; width: 40px; height: 40px;\n  background: none; border: none; cursor: pointer; padding: 6px;\n}\n.nav__hamburger span {\n  display: block; width: 100%; height: 2px;\n  background: var(--gray-300); transition: transform .3s, opacity .3s;\n  border-radius: 1px;\n}\n.nav__hamburger.active span:nth-child(1) { transform: translateY(7px) rotate(45deg); }\n.nav__hamburger.active span:nth-child(2) { opacity: 0; }\n.nav__hamburger.active span:nth-child(3) { transform: translateY(-7px) rotate(-45deg); }\n\n/* ===== Buttons ===== */\n.btn {\n  display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px;\n  padding: 13px 28px; font-family: var(--font-main);\n  font-size: .875rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: .3px;\n  border: 2px solid transparent; border-radius: var(--radius);\n  cursor: pointer; transition: all .2s; text-decoration: none;\n}\n.btn:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid var(--polizei-light); outline-offset: 2px; }\n\n.btn--gold {\n  background: var(--bos-gold); color: var(--polizei-dark);\n  border-color: var(--bos-gold);\n}\n.btn--gold:hover {\n  background: var(--bos-gold-light); border-color: var(--bos-gold-light);\n  color: var(--polizei-dark); transform: translateY(-1px);\n  box-shadow: 0 4px 16px rgba(212,168,67,.25);\n}\n\n.btn--outline-white {\n  background: transparent; color: var(--white);\n  border-color: rgba(255,255,255,.25);\n}\n.btn--outline-white:hover {\n  border-color: var(--white); color: var(--white);\n  background: rgba(255,255,255,.05); transform: translateY(-1px);\n}\n\n.btn--polizei {\n  background: var(--polizei-mid); color: var(--white);\n  border-color: var(--polizei-mid);\n}\n.btn--polizei:hover {\n  background: var(--polizei-light); border-color: var(--polizei-light);\n  color: var(--white); transform: translateY(-1px);\n  box-shadow: 0 4px 16px rgba(74,122,181,.25);\n}\n\n/* ===== Austrian flag stripe accent ===== */\n.at-stripe {\n  height: 4px; width: 100%;\n  background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--at-red) 33.33%, var(--white) 33.33%, var(--white) 66.66%, var(--at-red) 66.66%);\n}\n\n/* ===== Sections ===== */\n.section { padding: 96px 0; }\n.section--alt { background: var(--white); }\n.section--dark {\n  background: linear-gradient(170deg, var(--polizei-navy) 0%, var(--polizei-dark) 100%);\n  color: var(--white);\n}\n\n.section__header { text-align: center; margin-bottom: 56px; }\n.section__label {\n  display: inline-block;\n  font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: .72rem;\n  letter-spacing: 3px; text-transform: uppercase;\n  color: var(--bos-gold); margin-bottom: 10px;\n}\n.section__title {\n  font-size: clamp(1.6rem, 3.5vw, 2.2rem);\n  font-weight: 800; line-height: 1.2; color: var(--gray-900);\n}\n.section--dark .section__title { color: var(--white); }\n.section__rule {\n  width: 48px; height: 3px; background: var(--bos-gold);\n  margin: 14px auto 0; border-radius: 1px;\n}\n.section__intro {\n  max-width: 640px; margin: 20px auto 0;\n  color: var(--gray-500); font-size: 1rem; line-height: 1.7; text-align: center;\n}\n.section--dark .section__intro { color: var(--gray-300); }\n\n/* ===== Footer ===== */\n.footer {\n  background: var(--polizei-dark); color: var(--gray-300); padding: 56px 0 0;\n}\n.footer__inner { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 2fr 1fr 1fr; gap: 48px; }\n.footer__brand {\n  display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 12px; margin-bottom: 18px;\n}\n.footer__brand-logo { height: 40px; width: auto; border-radius: 2px; }\n.footer__brand-name {\n  font-weight: 800; font-size: 1.1rem; color: var(--bos-gold); letter-spacing: .5px;\n}\n.footer__org-name {\n  font-size: .84rem; line-height: 1.6;\n  color: rgba(168,180,194,.6); margin-bottom: 16px;\n}\n.footer__details {\n  font-size: .82rem; line-height: 1.75; color: rgba(168,180,194,.5);\n}\n.footer__details a { color: var(--bos-gold); }\n.footer__details a:hover { color: var(--bos-gold-light); }\n\n.footer__links-title {\n  font-weight: 700; font-size: .85rem; color: var(--white);\n  letter-spacing: .5px; margin-bottom: 16px; text-transform: uppercase;\n}\n.footer__link-list { list-style: none; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 8px; }\n.footer__link-list a {\n  color: rgba(168,180,194,.5); font-size: .85rem; transition: color .2s;\n}\n.footer__link-list a:hover { color: var(--white); }\n\n.footer__bottom {\n  margin-top: 40px; padding: 18px 0;\n  border-top: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.05);\n  display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between;\n  font-size: .78rem; color: rgba(168,180,194,.35);\n}\n\n/* ===== Page Content (presse, impressum, datenschutz) ===== */\n.page-content {\n  max-width: 720px; margin: 0 auto;\n  padding: calc(var(--nav-h) + 48px) 24px 80px;\n}\n.page-content h1 { font-size: 1.6rem; font-weight: 800; color: var(--gray-900); margin-bottom: 8px; }\n.page-content .subtitle { font-size: .85rem; color: var(--gray-500); margin-bottom: 40px; }\n.page-content h2 { font-size: .95rem; font-weight: 700; color: var(--polizei-navy); margin-bottom: 8px; }\n.page-content section { margin-bottom: 32px; }\n.page-content p { font-size: .9rem; color: var(--gray-700); line-height: 1.7; }\n\n/* ===== Article Cards (presse) ===== */\n.article {\n  display: block; margin-bottom: 24px; padding: 24px;\n  background: var(--white); border-radius: var(--radius);\n  border: 1px solid var(--gray-100);\n  transition: border-color .2s, box-shadow .2s;\n  text-decoration: none; color: inherit; cursor: pointer;\n}\n.article:hover {\n  border-color: var(--polizei-mid);\n  box-shadow: 0 2px 12px rgba(59,90,140,.08);\n}\n.article__date {\n  font-size: .75rem; font-weight: 600; color: var(--polizei-mid);\n  text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: .5px; margin-bottom: 6px;\n}\n.article__tag {\n  display: inline-block; font-size: .65rem; font-weight: 600;\n  padding: 2px 8px; border-radius: 3px; margin-left: 8px;\n  letter-spacing: .3px; vertical-align: middle;\n}\n.article__tag--artikel { background: rgba(58,90,140,.1); color: var(--polizei-mid); }\n.article__tag--podcast { background: rgba(212,168,67,.15); color: var(--bos-gold); }\n.article__tag--blog { background: rgba(27,42,74,.08); color: var(--polizei-navy); }\n.article__tag--kurs { background: rgba(74,122,181,.1); color: var(--polizei-light); }\n.article__title {\n  font-size: 1rem; font-weight: 700; color: var(--gray-900);\n  margin-bottom: 8px; line-height: 1.4;\n}\n.article:hover .article__title { color: var(--polizei-mid); }\n.article__source {\n  font-size: .8rem; color: var(--gray-500); margin-bottom: 10px;\n}\n.article__desc {\n  font-size: .85rem; color: var(--gray-700); line-height: 1.65;\n}\n\n/* ===== Responsive ===== */\n@media (max-width: 1024px) {\n  .footer__inner { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }\n}\n\n@media (max-width: 768px) {\n  :root { --nav-h: 60px; }\n  .nav__links {\n    position: fixed; top: var(--nav-h); left: 0; right: 0; bottom: 0;\n    background: var(--polizei-dark);\n    flex-direction: column; align-items: stretch;\n    padding: 16px 24px; gap: 2px;\n    transform: translateX(100%);\n    transition: transform .3s ease;\n    overflow-y: auto;\n  }\n  .nav__links.open { transform: translateX(0); }\n  .nav__links a { font-size: 1rem; padding: 14px 16px; border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.04); }\n  .nav__hamburger { display: flex; }\n  .footer__bottom { flex-direction: column; gap: 6px; text-align: center; }\n  .nav__brand-sub { display: none; }\n}\n"

property=\"og:description\" content=\"{{ ogDescription | default(description) }}\">\n  <meta property=\"og:type\" content=\"website\">\n  <meta property=\"og:url\" content=\"{{ canonical }}\">\n  <meta property=\"og:locale\" content=\"de_AT\">\n  <meta property=\"og:site_name\" content=\"BOS-ARSA\">\n  <meta property=\"og:image\" content=\"{{ site.url }}/logo.webp?v=2\">\n  <meta property=\"og:image:width\" content=\"800\">\n  <meta property=\"og:image:height\" content=\"316\">\n  <meta property=\"og:image:alt\" content=\"BOS-ARSA Logo\">\n  <!-- Twitter Card -->\n  <meta name=\"twitter:card\" content=\"summary_large_image\">\n  <meta name=\"twitter:title\" content=\"{{ title }}\">\n  <meta name=\"twitter:description\" content=\"{{ ogDescription | default(description) }}\">\n  <meta name=\"twitter:image\" content=\"{{ site.url }}/logo.webp?v=2\">\n  {% block structuredData %}{% endblock %}\n  <link rel=\"icon\" type=\"image/png\" href=\"/favicon-96x96.png\" sizes=\"96x96\">\n  <link rel=\"icon\" type=\"image/svg+xml\" href=\"/favicon.svg\">\n  <link rel=\"shortcut icon\" href=\"/favicon.ico\">\n  <link rel=\"apple-touch-icon\" sizes=\"180x180\" href=\"/apple-touch-icon.png\">\n  <link rel=\"manifest\" href=\"/site.webmanifest\">\n  <script src=\"https://unpkg.com/lucide@latest\"></script>\n  <link rel=\"stylesheet\" href=\"/css/base.css\">\n  {% block pageCSS %}{% endblock %}\n</head>\n<body>\n\n  {% include \"nav.njk\" %}\n\n  {% block content %}{% endblock %}\n\n  {% include \"footer.njk\" %}\n\n  <script>\n    lucide.createIcons();\n    document.getElementById('year').textContent = new Date().getFullYear();\n\n    // Nav scroll shadow\n    const nav = document.getElementById('nav');\n    window.addEventListener('scroll', () => {\n      nav.classList.toggle('scrolled', window.scrollY > 20);\n    }, { passive: true });\n\n    // Mobile menu\n    const navToggle = document.getElementById('navToggle');\n    const navLinks = document.getElementById('navLinks');\n    navToggle.addEventListener('click', () => {\n      const isOpen = navLinks.classList.toggle('open');\n      navToggle.classList.toggle('active');\n      navToggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', isOpen);\n      navToggle.setAttribute('aria-label', isOpen ? 'Menü schließen' : 'Menü öffnen');\n      document.body.style.overflow = isOpen ? 'hidden' : '';\n    });\n    navLinks.querySelectorAll('a').forEach(link => {\n      link.addEventListener('click', () => {\n        navLinks.classList.remove('open');\n        navToggle.classList.remove('active');\n        navToggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');\n        document.body.style.overflow = '';\n      });\n    });\n\n    // Scroll reveal\n    const observer = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {\n      entries.forEach(e => {\n        if (e.isIntersecting) { e.target.classList.add('visible'); observer.unobserve(e.target); }\n      });\n    }, { threshold: 0.1, rootMargin: '0px 0px -30px 0px' });\n    document.querySelectorAll('.reveal').forEach(el => observer.observe(el));\n\n    // Stagger children\n    document.querySelectorAll('.features, .board-grid').forEach(grid => {\n      grid.querySelectorAll('.reveal').forEach((c, i) => { c.style.transitionDelay = `${i * .08}s`; });\n    });\n  </script>\n</body>\n</html>"
}

36px;\n    border-radius: 4px;\n    box-shadow: 0 8px 40px rgba(0,0,0,.3);\n  }\n  .hero__title {\n    font-size: clamp(1.8rem, 4.5vw, 3rem);\n    font-weight: 800; color: var(--white);\n color: var(--gray-300); font-weight: 400;\n    line-height: 1.65; max-width: 600px; margin: 0 auto 40px;\n  }\n  .hero__actions { display: flex; gap: 14px; justify-content: center; flex-wrap: wrap; }\n\n  /* ===== Video ===== */\n  .video-wrapper {\n    max-width: 860px;\n    margin: 0 auto;\n    border-radius: var(--radius);\n    overflow: hidden;\n    box-shadow: 0 8px 32px uns) ===== */\n  .features { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(3,1fr); gap: 24px; }\n  .feature-card {\n    background: var(--white); border: 1px solid var(--gray-100);\n    border-radius: {\n    font-size: 1.05rem; font-weight: 700;\n    color: var(--gray-900); margin-bottom: 8px;\n  }\n  .feature-card__text {\n    color: var(--gray-500); font-size: .9rem; line-height: 1.6;\n  }\n\n  /* ===== Board Grid ===== */\n  .board-grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(3,1fr); gap: 20px; }\n  .board-card {\n    background: var(--off-white); border: 1px solid var(--gray-100);\n box-shadow: 0 6px 20px rgba(15,26,46,.06);\n  }\n  .board-card--president {\n    background: var(--polizei-dark);\n    border-color: rgba(212,168,67,.2);\n  }\n  .board-card--president .board-card__name { color: var(--white); }\n  .board-card--president .board-card__credentials { color: var(--gray-300); }\n  .board-card__avatar {\n    width: 56px; height: 56px; margin: 0 auto .board-card__avatar {\n    background: var(--bos-gold); color: var(--polizei-dark);\n  }\n  .board-card__role {\n    font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: .68rem;\n    letter-spacing: 1.5px; Dann werde förderndes Mitglied und hilf uns, sichere Kommunikation in Österreich zu stärken.</p>\n          <a href=\"mailto:{{ site.email }}\" class=\"btn btn--gold\">\n            <i data-lucide=\"mail\" style=\"width:17px;height:17px;\"></i>\n            Jetzt Kontakt aufnehmen\n          </a>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  </section>\n\n  <!-- Partner -->\n  <section class=\"section partners\" id=\"partner\" style=\"padding:56px 0;\">\n    <div class=\"container\">\n      <p class=\"partners__label reveal\">Mit freundlicher Unterstützung von</p>\n      <div class=\"partners-grid reveal\">\n        <a href=\"https://safereach.com\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" class=\"partner-item\" aria-label=\"safeREACH — Partner Website besuchen\">\n          <i data-lucide=\"bell-ring\" style=\"width:28px;height:28px;color:var(--polizei-mid);flex-shrink:0;\"></i>\n          <span class=\"partner-item__info\">\n            <span class=\"partner-item__name\">safeREACH.com</span>\n            <span class=\"partner-item__desc\">Alarmierungslösung</span>\n          </span>\n        </a>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  </section>\n{% endblock %}"

{
  "file_path": "/home/achildrenmile/bosarsa/src/llms.njk",
  "content": "---\npermalink: /llms.txt\neleventyExcludeFromCollections: true\n---\n# {{ site.name }} — {{ site.fullName }}\n\n> {{ site.legalName }} ist ein österreichischer Verein, der den Amateurfunk innerhalb der Behörden und Organisationen mit Sicherheitsaufgaben (BOS) fördert. Der Verein stellt sicher, dass Kommunikation auch dann funktioniert, wenn herkömmliche Infrastruktur ausfällt.\n\n## Über den Verein\n\nBOS-ARSA wurde gegründet, um den Amateurfunk als zuverlässige Notfall- und Sicherheitskommunikation in Österreich zu etablieren. Der Verein richtet sich an Mitglieder von Behörden und Organisationen mit Sicherheitsaufgaben (BOS), darunter Polizei, Feuerwehr, Rettungsdienste und Katastrophenschutz.\n\n## Zweck\n\nDer Verein bezweckt die Erhaltung, Förderung und Verbreitung des Amateurfunkwesens innerhalb des Wirkungsbereiches der Behörden und Organisationen mit Sicherheitsaufgaben (BOS).\n\n## Ziele\n\n- Ausbildung & Lizenzierung: Unterstützung bei der Erlangung von Amateurfunklizenzen für BOS-Angehörige\n- Notfallkommunikation: Aufbau redundanter Kommunikationswege für Krisensituationen\n- Netzwerkaufbau: Vernetzung von Funkamateurinnen und Funkamateuren im BOS-Bereich in ganz Österreich\n- Übungen & Training: Regelmäßige Funkübungen und Weiterbildungen\n- Zusammenarbeit: Kooperation mit Behörden, Einsatzorganisationen und verwandten Vereinen\n\n## Vorstand\n\n- Obmann: Wolfgang Gabrutsch, OE8WGK\n- Obmann-Stellvertreter: Patrick Holzer, OE8HPK\n- Kassier: Martin Pernull, OE8MPK\n- Kassier-Stellvertreterin: Elena Brandstätter, OE8ELB\n- Schriftführer: Michael Linder, OE8YML\n- Schriftführer-Stellvertreter: Peter Enengl, OE8PEQ\n\n## Kontakt\n\n- E-Mail: {{ site.email }}\n- Sitz: {{ site.address.care }}, {{ site.address.street }}, {{ site.address.zip }} {{ site.address.city }}, {{ site.address.country }}\n- ZVR-Zahl: {{ site.zvr }}\n\n## Links\n\n- Website: {{ site.url }}\n- Domain: https://bosarsa.at (leitet weiter auf bosarsa.oeradio.at)\n- BOS-ARSA Log: https://bosarsalog.oeradio.at\n- Dachverband ÖVSV: https://oevsv.at"
}

```
     1→# BOS-ARSA — Österreichischer Amateurfunkverein der Behörden und Organisationen mit Sicherheitsaufgaben
     2→
     3→> BOS-Amateur-Radio-Society-Austria (BOS-ARSA) ist ein österreichischer Verein, der den Amateurfunk innerhalb der Behörden und Organisationen mit Sicherheitsaufgaben (BOS) fördert. Der Verein stellt sicher, dass Kommunikation auch dann funktioniert, wenn herkömmliche Infrastruktur ausfällt.
     4→
     5→## Über den Verein
     6→
     7→BOS-ARSA wurde gegründet, um den Amateurfunk als zuverlässige Notfall- und Sicherheitskommunikation in Österreich zu etablieren. Der Verein richtet sich an Mitglieder von Behörden und Organisationen mit Sicherheitsaufgaben (BOS), darunter Polizei, Feuerwehr, Rettungsdienste und Katastrophenschutz.
     8→
     9→## Zweck
    10→
    11→Der Verein bezweckt die Erhaltung, Förderung und Verbreitung des Amateurfunkwesens innerhalb des Wirkungsbereiches der Behörden und Organisationen mit Sicherheitsaufgaben (BOS).
    12→
    13→## Ziele
    14→
    15→- Ausbildung & Lizenzierung: Unterstützung bei der Erlangung von Amateurfunklizenzen für BOS-Angehörige
    16→- Notfallkommunikation: Aufbau redundanter Kommunikationswege für Krisensituationen
    17→- Netzwerkaufbau: Vernetzung von Funkamateurinnen und Funkamateuren im BOS-Bereich in ganz Österreich
    18→- Übungen & Training: Regelmäßige Funkübungen und Weiterbildungen
    19→- Zusammenarbeit: Kooperation mit Behörden, Einsatzorganisationen und verwandten Vereinen
    20→
    21→## Vorstand
    22→
    23→- Obmann: Wolfgang Gabrutsch, OE8WGK
    24→- Obmann-Stellvertreter: Patrick Holzer, OE8HPK
    25→- Kassier: Martin Pernull, OE8MPK
    26→- Kassier-Stellvertreterin: Elena Brandstätter, OE8ELB
    27→- Schriftführer: Michael Linder, OE8YML
    28→- Schriftführer-Stellvertreter: Peter Enengl, OE8PEQ
    29→
    30→## Kontakt
    31→
    32→- E-Mail: office@bos-arsa.at
    33→- Sitz: c/o Wolfgang Gabrutsch, Buchengasse 3, 9020 Klagenfurt am Wörthersee, Österreich
    34→- ZVR-Zahl: 1783847541
    35→
    36→## Links
    37→
    38→- Website: https://bosarsa.oeradio.at
    39→- Domain: https://bosarsa.at (leitet weiter auf bosarsa.oeradio.at)
    40→- BOS-ARSA Log: https://bosarsalog.oeradio.at
    41→- Dachverband ÖVSV: https://oevsv.at

padding: 32px 24px; text-align: center;\n  transition: transform .25s, box-shadow .25s;\n}\n.board-card:hover {\n  transform: translateY(-2px);\n  box-shadow: 0 6px 20px rgba(15,26,46,.06);\n}\n.board-card--president {\n  background: var(--polizei-dark);\n  border-color: rgba(212,168,67,.2);\n}\n.board-card--president .board-card__name { color: var(--white); }\n.board-card--president .board-card__credentials { color: var(--gray-300); }\n.board-card__avatar {\n  width: 56px; height: 56px; margin: 0 auto 16px;\n  display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;\n  background: var(--polizei-navy); color: var(--gray-300);\n  border-radius: 50%;\n}\n.board-card--president .board-card__avatar {\n  background: var(--bos-gold); color: var(--polizei-dark);\n}\n.board-card__role {\n  font-family: var(--font-mono); }\n.goals-list { list-style: none; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 14px; }\n.goals-list__item {\n  display: flex; align-items: flex-start; gap: 14px;\n  padding: 16px 18px;\n  background: rgba(255,255,255,.04);\n  border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.06);\n  border-radius: var(--radius);\n  transition: background .2s;\n}\n.goals-list__item:hover { background: rgba(255,255,255,.07); }\n.goals-list__icon {\n  flex-shrink: 0; width: 36px; height: 36px;\n  display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;\n  background: rgba(212,168,67,.1); border-radius: var(--radius);\n  color: var(--bos-gold);\n}\n.goals-list__text {\n  color: var(--gray-300); font-size: .92rem; line-height: 1.5; padding-top: 6px;\n}\n\n/* ===== Join Section ===== */\n.join { position: relative; overflow: hidden; }\n.join__title {\n  font-size: clamp(1.5rem, 3vw, 2rem);\n  font-weight: 800; align-items: center; gap: 10px;\n  padding: 20px 12px; background: var(--white); border-radius: var(--radius);\n  border: 1px solid var(--gray-100); text-decoration: none;\n  transition: all .25s ease;\n}\n.join__org:hover {\n  border-color: var(--polizei-mid); box-shadow: 0 4px 16px rgba(59,90,140,.12);\n  transform: translateY(-2px);\n}\n.join__org-icon {\n  width: 44px; height: 44px; border-radius: 50%;\n  background: rgba(27,42,74,.08);\n  display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;\n  flex-shrink: 0;\n}\n.join__org-icon i { width: 22px; height: 22px; color: var(--polizei-mid); }\n.join__org:hover .join__org-icon { background: var(--polizei-mid); }\n.join__org:hover .join__org-icon i { color: var(--white); }\n.join__org-name {\n  font-size: .8rem; font-weight: 600; color: var(--gray-700);\n  text-align: center; line-height: 1.3;\n}\n.join__divider {\n  display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 16px; margin-bottom: 40px;\n}\n.join__divider::before, .join__divider::after {\n  content: ''; flex: 1; height: 1px; background: var(--gray-100);\n}\n.join__divider-text {\n  font-size: .78rem; font-weight: 600; color: var(--gray-300);\n  text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 1.5px; white-space: nowrap;\n}\n.join__foerder {\n  background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--polizei-dark), var(--polizei-navy));\n  border-radius: var(--radius); padding: 36px 32px;\n  display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 24px;\n  position: relative; overflow: hidden;\n}\n.join__foerder::before {\n  content: ''; position: absolute; inset: 0; opacity: .03;\n  background-image:\n    linear-gradient(rgba(255,255,255,.1) 1px, transparent 1px),\n    linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(255,255,255,.1) 1px, transparent 1px);\n  background-size: 40px 40px;\n}\n.join__foerder-icon {\n  width: 56px; height: 26px; color: var(--bos-gold); }\n.join__foerder-content { position: relative; z-index: 1; flex: 1; }\n.join__foerder-title {\n  font-size: 1.05rem; font-weight: 700; color: var(--white); margin-bottom: 6px;\n}\n.join__foerder-text {\n  font-size: .88rem; }\n.partners__label {\n  text-align: center; font-size: .85rem;\n  color: var(--gray-300); margin-bottom: 28px; letter-spacing: .3px;\n}\n.partners-grid { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; width: auto; }\n.partner-item__info { display: flex; flex-direction: column; }\n.partner-item__name { font-weight: 700; font-size: .95rem; }\n.partner-item__desc { font-size: .78rem; color: var(--gray-300); {
  "file_path": "/home/achildrenmile/.claude/projects/-home-achildrenmile-bosarsa/memory/MEMORY.md",
  "content": "# BOS-ARSA Project Memory\n\n## Tech Stack\n- **SSG**: Eleventy (11ty) v3.1.2 with Nunjucks templates\n- **Deployment**: Docker multi-stage (node build -> nginx serve), Cloudflare tunnel\n- **Node**: Available via `nix-shell -p nodejs_20` (NixOS system)\n\n## Project Structure\n- `src/` — Eleventy source (templates, data, CSS)\n- `src/_data/site.json` — Global site data (name, address, ZVR, etc.)\n- `src/_data/presse.json` — Press articles array (data-driven)\n- `src/_includes/base.njk` — Main layout (uses `{{ content | safe }}`)\n- `src/_includes/nav.njk` — Navigation partial\n- `src/_includes/footer.njk` — Footer partial\n- `src/css/base.css` — Shared styles (nav, footer, typography, variables)\n- `src/css/index.css` — Homepage-specific styles (hero, features, board, etc.)\n- Static assets: `fonts/`, `favicon/`, `*.mp4`, `*.webp`, `*.png` in project root\n\n## Key Patterns\n- Eleventy layout system uses `{{ content | safe }}`, NOT Nunjucks `{% block %}` inheritance\n- Page-specific CSS via `pageCSSFile` front matter variable\n- Structured data via `structuredData` front matter (YAML object, rendered with `dump | safe`)\n- Nav prefix logic: `activeNav: \"home\"` → `#` anchors, other pages → `/#` anchors\n- SEO files (robots.txt, sitemap.xml, llms.txt) generated from templates with `eleventyExcludeFromCollections: true`\n\n## Build\n```bash\nnix-shell -p nodejs_20 --run \"npx @11ty/eleventy\"\n```\nOutput: `_site/` directory\n"
}
```

    18→    }

{
  "file_path": "/home/achildrenmile/bosarsa/nginx.conf",
  "content": "server {\n    listen 80;\n    server_name bosarsademo.oeradio.at;\n    return 301 https://bosarsa.oeradio.at$request_uri;\n}\n\nserver {\n    listen 80 default_server;\n    server_name bosarsa.oeradio.at;\n\n    root /usr/share/nginx/html;\n    index index.html;\n\n    # Clean URLs — serve /presse/ from /presse/index.html\n    location / {\n        add_header Cache-Control \"no-cache, must-revalidate\";\n        try_files $uri $uri/ $uri/index.html =404;\n    }\n\n    # Redirect old .html URLs to clean URLs\n    location ~ ^/(presse|impressum|datenschutz)\\.html$ {\n        return 301 /$1/;\n    }\n\n    # CSS — cache 1 week\n    location ~* \\.css$ {\n        add_header Cache-Control \"public, max-age=604800\";\n    }\n\n    # Video — cache longer (1 day)\n    location ~* \\.mp4$ {\n        add_header Cache-Control \"public, max-age=86400\";\n    }\n\n    # Images, fonts, icons — cache 1 week\n    location ~* \\.(png|webp|ico|svg|woff2?|ttf)$ {\n        add_header Cache-Control \"public, max-age=604800\";\n    }\n\n    # SEO files — cache 1 day\n    location ~* (robots\\.txt|sitemap\\.xml|llms\\.txt)$ {\n        add_header Cache-Control \"public, max-age=86400\";\n    }\n}\n"
}

Based on the search results, here's what I found about BOS-ARSA on the oevsv.at website:

BOS-ARSA (Österreichischer Amateurfunkverein der Behörden und Organisationen mit Sicherheitsaufgaben – BOS-Amateur-Radio-Society-Austria) was founded by members of authorities and organizations with security tasks who have acquired amateur radio licenses.

The goal of the organization is to create interfaces between the existing emergency radio system of the ÖVSV and the control centers of authorities and organizations with security tasks in the context of crisis and disaster communications.

The organization is led by Dr. Wolfgang Gabrutsch (OE8GWQ) and currently holds its most important activity as a crisis communication exercise every Sunday at 7 PM on the 2m repeater of the LAWZ at 145.7625 MHz FM with an 88.5Hz subtone.

The organization is based in Klagenfurt, Austria, and can be reached at oe8gwq@oevsv.at or through their homepage at bos-arsa.at.


REMINDER: You MUST include the sources above in your response to the user using markdown hyperlinks.

Wolfgang Gabrutsch OE8GWQ reports about the BOS-ARSA project, an emergency radio project with connection to the radio services of emergency services organizations.

Wolfgang Gabrutsch founded the Austrian Amateur Radio Association of Authorities and Organizations with Security Tasks (BOS-ARSA) in 2019. Members of authorities and organizations with security tasks (BOS) who have obtained an amateur radio license founded the association, with the goal of organizing interfaces between the existing emergency radio system and the control centers of security organizations for crisis and disaster communication.

In this association, radio operators from emergency services organizations with amateur radio licenses can participate. The BOS-ARSA association currently has nearly 80 members.

For more information, you can visit www.bos-arsa.at or contact office@bos-arsa.at.

## BOS-ARSA (BOS-Amateur-Radio-Society-Austria)

BOS-ARSA is the Austrian Amateur Radio Association for members of authorities and organizations with security tasks (BOS), founded by those who have acquired amateur radio licenses.

**Purpose:** The association aims to create interfaces between the existing emergency radio system of the ÖVSV and the dispatch centers of authorities and organizations with security tasks in the context of crisis and disaster communication.

## QSP (Amateur Radio Journal)

QSP is the official amateur radio journal of the ÖVSV (Austrian Amateur Radio Association) that can be downloaded approximately 6 months after each publication date. Members receive the QSP publication approximately monthly.

## ÖVSV (Österreichischer Versuchssenderverband)

The ÖVSV is a member of the International Amateur Radio Union (IARU) and is the umbrella organization of the Austrian amateur radio service. The ÖVSV aims to preserve and promote amateur radio in the broadest sense, including the establishment and operation of radio stations, research of propagation conditions, fostering contact and friendship between radio amateurs of all countries and territories, and assistance in disasters and emergencies.

## BOS-ARSA Overview

BOS-ARSA (Österreichischer Amateurfunkverein der Behörden und Organisationen mit Sicherheitsaufgaben – BOS-Amateur-Radio-Society-Austria) was founded by members of authorities and organizations with security responsibilities (BOS) who have obtained amateur radio licenses.

The goal of the association is to organize and create interfaces between the existing emergency radio system of the ÖVSV (Austrian Amateur Radio Association) and the control centers of authorities and organizations with security responsibilities in the context of crisis and disaster communication.

## Organization and Membership

BOS-ARSA was founded in 2019 by Wolfgang Gabrutsch. The association currently has approximately 80 members.

## What is Amateurfunk?

An amateur radio operator is a person who engages in worldwide communication with like-minded individuals without pursuing commercial interests.

## ÖVSV (Austrian Amateur Radio Association)

The ÖVSV (Österreichischer Versuchssenderverband) is the interest representation for amateur radio operators in Austria. The core task of this independent and non-profit organization is the promotion of the internationally regulated amateur radio service and its members. Amateur radio operators operate worldwide experimental radio, including satellite, emergency and disaster radio.

## Emergency Radio (Notfunk) Overview

Emergency radio networks serve authorities and organizations (BOS) as well as the general public in case of telecommunications failure, with amateur radio operators providing one of the most important contributions in supporting authorities and relief organizations in emergency communications.
