```
# BOS-ARSA Information Summary

**Organization Name & Purpose:**
"Österreichischen Amateurfunkverein der Behörden und Organisationen mit Sicherheitsaufgaben – BOS-Amateur-Radio-Society-Austria" - an organization for licensed amateur radio operators from security and emergency services.

**Main Objective:**
The group aims to organize connections between ÖVSV's emergency communications system and command centers of security organizations for crisis and disaster communication.

**Leadership:**
Dr. Wolfgang Gabrutsch (OE8GWQ) serves as President and local section leader (ADL 820).

**Contact Information:**
- Email: oe8gwq@oevsv.at
- Address: Buchengasse 3, 9020 Klagenfurt am Wörthersee
- Homepage: http://www.bos-arsa.at/

**Activities:**
Weekly "Krisenkommunikationsübung" (crisis communication exercise) every Sunday at 19:00 on the 2m repeater 145.7625 MHz FM with 88.5 Hz subton.

**Links:**
- Main section page: http://oe8.oevsv.at/ortsstellen/adl820/
- BOS-ARSA website: http://www.bos-arsa.at/

**Associated Images:**
One photo from BOS-ARSA action event in Ferlach 2020 (credited to OE8DBK).
```

```
BOS-ARSA (BOS-Amateur-Radio-Society-Austria) was founded by members of authorities and organizations with security responsibilities (BOS) who have acquired amateur radio licenses. The organization's goal is to organize and create interfaces between the existing emergency radio system and the control centers of security organizations as part of crisis and disaster communication.

Due to the need for alternative communication options in case of a blackout or other failure of critical infrastructure, BOS-ARSA has a permanent place in emergency services coordination. In emergency and disaster situations, amateur radio operators would operate radio stations in the crisis command centers to ensure communication with emergency radio operators in community "light islands" to provide support to the population in case conventional communication systems fail.
```

```
Based on the search results, I found information about BOS-ARSA and ADL 820 related to emergency radio (Notfunk) activities in Carinthia (Kärnten), Austria:

## Overview

BOS-ARSA (Austrian Amateur Radio Society of Authorities and Organizations with Security Tasks) is a group that has established itself as its own local chapter ADL 820. The BOS-ARSA, also known as local chapter ADL 820, has dedicated itself to training and educating members of security authorities starting from Carinthia.

## Purpose and Activities

The association's goal is to organize and create interfaces between the existing emergency radio system of ÖVSV and the control centers of security authorities within crisis and disaster communication. Members of rescue organizations, fire departments, lifeguards, and police are trained as radio amateurs and help in emergencies using amateur radio means.

## Regular Exercises

The most important activity is the crisis communication exercise every Sunday at 19:00 on the LAWZ 2m repeater on 145.7625 MHz FM with 88.5 Hz subaudible tone.

The search results did not contain specific information about exercises in 2022, 2023, or 2024. For more detailed information about exercises from those specific years, you may want to contact Dr. Wolfgang Gabrutsch OE8GWQ, who is listed as the president and contact person for ADL 820.
```

```
Based on the search results, here's what I found about BOS-ARSA and ADL820:

## BOS-ARSA (Austrian Organization)

BOS-ARSA is the "Österreichischer Amateurfunkverein der Behörden und Organisationen mit Sicherheitsaufgaben – BOS-Amateur-Radio-Society-Austria," an organization founded by members of Austrian authorities and security organizations (BOS) who have obtained amateur radio licenses.

## ADL 820

ADL 820 is a local section/club affiliated with BOS-ARSA, with President Dr. Wolfgang Gabrutsch based at the Regional Police Directorate of Carinthia in Klagenfurt. The organization's main activity is a crisis communication exercise held every Sunday at 19:00 on the 2m repeater at 145.7625 MHz FM with 88.5 Hz subtone.

## Key Distinction

It's important to note that BOS and amateur radio (AFu) are two completely different things – a BOS license is not sufficient for amateur radio operations; a separate amateur radio license is required. However, BOS-ARSA specifically bridges these worlds by bringing together individuals from security organizations who have obtained amateur radio licenses.

The search results show that BOS-ARSA and ADL 820 are Austrian-based organizations, not German ones, focusing on amateur radio operations for emergency and crisis communication.
```

```
Based on the web search results, here's what I found about BOS-ARSA and the related topics:

## BOS-ARSA Overview

Wolfgang Gabrutsch founded the BOS-ARSA (Austrian Amateur Radio Association of Authorities and Organizations with Security Tasks) in 2019. It is an emergency radio project with connections to the radio services of blue light organizations.

## Purpose and Crisis Communication

The organization's goal is to create interfaces between the existing emergency radio system and the control centers of authorities and organizations with security tasks in the context of crisis and disaster communication. Due to the need for alternative communication options in case of a blackout or other failure of critical infrastructure, BOS-ARSA has a fixed place at blue light meetings.

## Activities and Training

The most important activity is the crisis communication exercise every Sunday at 7 p.m., starting on the 2m repeater of LAWZ at 145.7625 MHz FM; 88.5 Hz subaudible tone.

## Membership

The BOS-ARSA association currently has approximately 80 members.

I found no specific search results mentioning Ferlach in connection with BOS-ARSA, so I cannot provide information about that location in relation to this project based on these search results.
```

```
## Herbert Saurugg - Blackout and Crisis Preparedness Expert

Herbert Saurugg is an internationally recognized expert for blackout and crisis preparedness and president of the Society for Crisis Preparedness. He is the president of the Society for Crisis Preparedness (GfKV), an international blackout and crisis preparedness expert, keynote speaker, and idealist.

Herbert Saurugg has conducted numerous process consultations, workshops for preparation for a possible blackout, and trainings with blackout simulation for municipalities and crisis management teams called "Neustart".

## BOS Reference

In the context of Austrian emergency response, the communication network of the Austrian Ministry of the Interior (Digitalfunk-BOS Austria) works in a specially harmonized and standardized frequency band for crisis communication.

However, the search results don't contain specific details about "BOS-ARSA" as a formal cooperation or partnership. If you're looking for more specific information about a particular cooperation or initiative, you may want to contact Herbert Saurugg directly or visit the Society for Crisis Preparedness (GfKV) website.
```

```
Based on the search results, here's what I found about BOS-ARSA:

Wolfgang Gabrutsch founded BOS-ARSA (Österreichischer Amateurfunkverein der Behörden und Organisationen mit Sicherheitsaufgaben – Austrian Amateur Radio Society of Authorities and Organizations with Security Tasks) in 2019.

The association was founded by members of authorities and organizations with security tasks (BOS) who hold amateur radio licenses, with the goal of organizing and creating interfaces between the existing emergency radio system of the ÖVSV and the control centers of authorities and organizations with security tasks in the context of crisis and disaster communication.

Dr. Wolfgang Gabrutsch (OE8GWQ) serves as the association's president. BOS-ARSA is an emergency radio project with connections to the radio services of the emergency services organizations (blue light organizations).
```

```
### 1. MeinBezirk.at -- "Kurzwelle & Co: Die Welt der Funkamateure"

- **Source:** MeinBezirk.at (Klagenfurt Land)

- **Description:** A feature article about amateur radio in Carinthia, profiling Oberst Wolfgang Gabrutsch from Feistritz im Rosental. Reports that Gabrutsch founded BOS-ARSA in 2019, the association has approximately 80 members, and recently joined the OVSV umbrella organization. Discusses his callsign OE8GWQ, emergency preparedness during blackout scenarios, and the role of amateur radio in crisis communication.
- **Type:** presse

---

- **Description:** Reports that eight rescue workers from Austrian Water Rescue Carinthia completed an amateur radio course organized by BOS-ARSA at the Red Cross rescue station in Ferlach. The five-day course covered technical fundamentals, operating procedures, and legal requirements. Participants passed the CEPT-License exam, investing approximately 50 volunteer hours.
- **Type:** verband
```

```
# Article Summary: "Funken im Katastrophenfall"

**Publication Date:** October 5-6, 2021

**Key Points:**

Eight water rescue personnel from Austrian Water Rescue (Österreichische Wasserrettung) Carinthia completed an amateur radio course and passed the official exam in September 2021.

**Training Details:**
The course lasted five days and was organized by BOS-ARSA, described as "Österreichischer Amateurfunkverein der Behörden und Organisationen mit Sicherheitsaufgaben" (Austrian amateur radio association for authorities and safety organizations). The training took place at the Red Cross emergency station in Ferlach.

**Course Content:**
"Lehrinhalte waren u.a. technische Grundlagen, Funk- und Betriebstechnik sowie rechtliche Bestimmungen" (course content included technical fundamentals, radio and operating technology, and legal regulations).

**Practical Application:**
The newly trained operators will primarily be deployed during disaster response operations and for state or federal water rescue operations.

**Commitment:**
Participants invested approximately 50 volunteer hours in this training and certification program.
```

```
Based on the search results, I found information about the **BOS-ARSA and Emergency Communication (Notfunk) Technical Discussion in Carinthia (Kärnten)**.

The discussion was held as part of reports from district officials representing the fire department, the Austrian Red Cross, the Austrian Armed Forces, the KNG-Kärnten Netz GmbH, and the wastewater association, during which the goals of BOS-ARSA were presented by its president.

Key outcomes from this discussion include:

- Due to the need for alternative communication options in case of blackouts or other failures of critical infrastructure, BOS-ARSA received a permanent spot at the "Blaulicht-Stammtisch" (emergency services regular meeting).

- In the operations command center of the Völkermarkt district authority (BH Völkermarkt), the establishment of an amateur radio club station is planned, which will be staffed by BOS amateur radio operators during crises and disasters and will operate in the area of the message collection point.

- This will ensure communication with emergency radio operators in the light islands of communities, so that in case of failure of conventional communication means, appropriate support can be provided to the population.
```

```
Based on the web search results, I found information about BOS-ARSA and amateur radio in Austria, though the specific article from kleinezeitung.at about a security vulnerability doesn't appear in these results. However, I can provide you with what I found:

BOS-ARSA (Österreichischer Amateurfunkverein der Behörden und Organisationen mit Sicherheitsaufgaben – BOS-Amateur-Radio-Society-Austria) was founded by members of emergency and security organizations (BOS) who hold amateur radio licenses, with the goal of organizing interfaces between the existing emergency radio system of ÖVSV and the dispatch centers of BOS organizations in crisis and disaster communication.

Wolfgang Gabrutsch founded the Austrian Amateur Radio Association of Authorities and Organizations with Security Tasks (BOS-ARSA) in 2019. The association currently has approximately 80 members.

Unfortunately, the search results don't contain the specific kleinezeitung.at article about a security vulnerability (Sicherheitslücke) in BOS-ARSA from 2024. To find that specific article, you may want to try searching directly on the kleinezeitung.at website or adjusting your search terms.
```

```
# Article Summary

**Topic:** This article discusses how amateur radio operators serve as a critical communication backup when standard infrastructure fails during emergencies like earthquakes or blackouts.

**Key Figure:** The article focuses on Alfons Jöbstl (callsign OE8AJK), an Austrian amateur radio operator who participated in a blackout drill in the Lavanttal region.

**Date:** March 18, 2019

**Specific Mentions:** The article does not mention BOS-ARSA, Gabrutsch, or OE8GWQ. It references approximately 6,000 amateur radio operators in Austria and discusses their role in emergency communication, including historical examples from the 1976 Friuli earthquake and 2014 ice storm in Slovenia.

**Notable Quote:** "Wir sind in der Lage, mit recht einfachen Mitteln wie einer aufgeladenen Autobatterie und einem Kurzwellenfunkgerät die Verbindung trotz Blackout aufrecht zu erhalten" (Amateur radio operators can maintain connections despite blackouts using simple means like charged car batteries and shortwave equipment).
```

```
Perfect! I have all the information you need. Let me provide you with a comprehensive analysis of the BOS-ARSA website structure.

## BOS-ARSA Homepage Structure - Complete Analysis

### Homepage Sections in Order

The homepage (`/index.njk`) consists of the following sections in this order:

| Order | Section ID | Section Title | CSS Class | Design Pattern |
|-------|-----------|---------------|-----------|-----------------|
| 1 | `#hero` | Hero/Header | `.hero` | Dark background, animated waves, logo, CTA buttons |
| 2 | Austrian flag stripe | Visual separator | `.at-stripe` | Decorative element |
| 3 | `#ueber-uns` | Über uns (About Us) | `.section` | Light background with 3-column feature cards |
| 4 | `#video` | Videos | `.section section--alt` | Light background with video embeds |
| 5 | `#vorstand` | Unser Vorstand (Board) | `.section` | Light background with 3-column board member cards |
| 6 | `#ziele` | Unsere Ziele (Our Goals) | `.section section--dark` | Dark background, split layout with goals list |
| 7 | `#mitglied` | Mitglied werden (Join) | `.section join` | Light background, organization icons grid, dark gradient box |
| 8 | `#partner` | Partner | `.section partners` | White background with partner logos |

---

### Navigation Structure

The navigation (`nav.njk`) includes these links:

```
Über uns       (#ueber-uns)
Videos         (#video)
Vorstand       (#vorstand)
Ziele          (#ziele)
Mitglied werden (#mitglied)
Presse         (/presse/) — External page
Kontakt        (#kontakt) — This section doesn't exist on homepage yet!
```

Note: The navigation references a `#kontakt` anchor but there is no Contact section on the current homepage.

---

### CSS Design Patterns & Section Styles

#### Dark Sections Pattern
- **`.section--dark`** (Goals section): Dark navy/blue background (`var(--polizei-dark)`)
- White text content
- Gold accent colors for highlighted text
- Semi-transparent icon backgrounds with gold icons

#### Light Section Pattern
- **`.section`** (default): White/light background
- Dark gray text
- Subtle borders and shadows

#### Card Layouts

**Feature Cards** (`.feature-card` - About Us section):
- Grid: 3 columns (responsive to 2 at 1024px, 1 at 768px)
- White background with subtle border
- Icon in navy circle with gold icon
- Hover effect: Yellow underline animates from left
- Gap: 24px

**Board Cards** (`.board-card` - Board section):
- Grid: 3 columns (responsive to 2 at 1024px, 1 centered at 768px)
- Off-white background
- Avatar circle (navy with gray icon, or gold with navy icon for president)
- Hover effect: Slight lift with shadow
- President card has dark background with gold accents

**Organization Icons** (`.join__org` - Join section):
- Grid: 4 columns (responsive to 2 at smaller screens)
- Circular icon backgrounds (navy-tinted)
- Hover effect: Icon color becomes navy, background changes to navy with white icon
- 8 organizations displayed

---

### Section-Specific CSS Details

#### Hero Section (`.hero`)
```
- Background: var(--polizei-dark) — Dark blue
- Animated wave pattern with pulsing circles
- Grid background overlay
- Logo: 420px width, max 80vw
- CTA Buttons: Gold and white outline
- Padding: calc(var(--nav-h) + 48px) 24px 80px
```

#### Goals Section (`.section--dark`)
```
- 2-column grid layout (responsive to 1 column)
- Gap: 56px
- Left: Large statement text (1.25-1.65rem, white, gold highlights)
- Right: List of 4 items with icons
- List items: Semi-transparent backgrounds (rgba(255,255,255,.04))
- Icon boxes: Rounded, gold-tinted background
```

#### Join Section (`.join`)
```
- Title: Large (1.5-2rem, dark gray, 800 weight)
- Subtitle: Center-aligned, max-width 680px
- Organization grid: 4 columns of icon buttons
- Divider: Horizontal line with "oder" text
- Supporting member box: Dark gradient background (linear-gradient 135deg)
  - Subtle grid pattern overlay
  - Icon circle with gold background
  - Button styling
```

#### Video Wrapper (`.video-wrapper`)
```
- Max-width: 860px
- Border-radius: var(--radius)
- Border: 1px solid var(--gray-100)
- Box-shadow: 0 8px 32px rgba(15,26,46,.12)
- Dark background for fallback
```

---

### Color Variables Used

From the CSS:
- `var(--polizei-dark)` — Very dark blue
- `var(--polizei-navy)` — Navy blue  
- `var(--polizei-mid)` — Medium blue
- `var(--bos-gold)` — Gold accent color
- `var(--white)` — White
- `var(--gray-300)`, `var(--gray-500)`, `var(--gray-900)` — Text colors
- `var(--off-white)` — Slightly off-white

---

### Responsive Breakpoints

- **1024px**: Cards go 2 columns, goals grid becomes 1 column
- **768px**: Cards go 1 column, section padding reduces, organization grid becomes 2 columns

---

### Key Observations for Your "Krisenkommunikation" Section

1. **No Schedule/Frequency Display**: The current site has no timeline or frequency-based content displays. This would be a new pattern to introduce.

2. **Existing Patterns to Match**:
   - Use `.section--dark` for a professional, serious appearance (appropriate for crisis exercises)
   - Use feature cards or goal list items for displaying multiple exercises
   - Consider a 3-column grid like "Über uns" for exercise cards if you need multiple items

3. **Best Placement**: This section would logically fit:
   - After the Ziele (#ziele) section and before Mitglied (#mitglied) — to show activities
   - Or after Vorstand to show board activities
   - Update nav.njk to include `#krisenkommunikation` link

4. **Suggested ID**: `id="krisenkommunikation"` matching the naming convention

5. **Design Considerations**:
   - Could use the dark section pattern (`.section--dark`) for visual weight
   - Could display exercises as cards (similar to feature cards or board cards)
   - Could include frequency information in a structured list (like goals list)
   - Gold accents would tie in with existing design
```

```json
  "old_string": "# Plan: Migrate BOS-ARSA to Eleventy (11ty)\n\n## Context\n\nThe BOS-ARSA website has 4 HTML pages with massive code duplication — nav, footer, CSS variables, font-face declarations, favicons, and JS are copy-pasted across every file. SEO files (robots.txt, sitemap.xml, llms.txt) are also manually maintained.\n\n**Goal:** Migrate to Eleventy (11ty) — a zero-config static site generator that outputs plain HTML. The nginx/Docker deployment stays identical, we just add a build step.\n\n## Why Eleventy\n\n- Zero-config, minimal dependencies (~1 npm package)\n- Nunjucks templating: layouts, partials, loops, data files\n- Output is plain static HTML — served by nginx exactly as before\n- JSON data files for press articles (add article = add JSON entry)\n- Can generate sitemap.xml, robots.txt, llms.txt from templates\n- No client-side framework — same HTML output as today\n\n## New Project Structure\n\n```\nbosarsa/\n├── src/\n│   ├── _includes/\n│   │   ├── base.njk              # Full HTML document shell\n│   │   ├── nav.njk               # Full navigation (logo, links, hamburger)\n│   │   └── footer.njk            # Full 3-column footer\n│   ├── _data/\n│   │   ├── site.json             # Global: name, url, email, address, ZVR, etc.\n│   │   └── presse.json           # Press articles array\n│   ├── css/\n│   │   └── base.css              # All shared CSS (vars, fonts, nav, footer, responsive)\n│   ├── index.njk                 # Homepage\n│   ├── presse.njk                # Press page (loops over presse.json)\n│   ├── impressum.njk             # Impressum (content only, layout from base)\n│   ├── datenschutz.njk           # Datenschutz (content only, layout from base)\n│   ├── robots.njk                # -> robots.txt\n│   ├── sitemap.njk               # -> sitemap.xml (auto-lists all pages)\n│   └── llms.njk                  # -> llms.txt (pulls from site.json)\n├── fonts/                         # Stays in root (passthrough copy)\n├── favicon/                       # Stays in root (passthrough copy)\n├── *.mp4, *.png, *.webp          # Static assets (passthrough copy)\n├── .eleventy.js                   # Eleventy config\n├── package.json                   # Just @11ty/eleventy\n├── Dockerfile                     # Multi-stage: node build -> nginx serve\n├── docker-compose.yml             # Unchanged\n├── deploy-production.sh           # Unchanged\n└── nginx.conf                     # Unchanged\n```\n\n## Key Files\n\n### 1. `src/_data/site.json` — Single source of truth\n```json\n{\n  \"name\": \"BOS-ARSA\",\n  \"fullName\": \"Österreichischer Amateurfunkverein der Behörden und Organisationen mit Sicherheitsaufgaben\",\n  \"legalName\": \"BOS-Amateur-Radio-Society-Austria (BOS-ARSA)\",\n  \"url\": \"https://bosarsa.oeradio.at\",\n  \"email\": \"office@bos-arsa.at\",\n  \"address\": { ... },\n  \"zvr\": \"1783847541\",\n  \"vereinsbehoerde\": \"Landespolizeidirektion Kärnten\"\n}\n```\n\n### 2. `src/_data/presse.json` — Data-driven press articles\n```json\n[\n  {\n    \"date\": \"22. April 2022\",\n    \"tag\": \"blog\",\n    \"tagLabel\": \"Blog\",\n    \"title\": \"Not- und Katastrophenfunk — ...\",\n    \"url\": \"https://www.mh-is.at/?p=1158\",\n    \"source\": \"mh-is.at — OE3EGH und OE3XEG\",\n    \"desc\": \"Ausführlicher Überblick...\"\n  }\n]\n```\n\n### 3. `src/_includes/base.njk` — Single layout for all pages\n- `<head>` with fonts, CSS, favicons, SEO meta (title, description, canonical, OG, Twitter)\n- Includes `nav.njk` and `footer.njk`\n- Content block for page-specific content\n- Shared JS at bottom (lucide, year, hamburger, scroll)\n- Front matter controls: `title`, `description`, `canonical`, `activeNav`, `pageCSS`\n\n### 4. `src/presse.njk` — Loop replaces 6 copy-pasted cards\n```nunjucks\n{% for article in presse %}\n  <a class=\"article\" href=\"{{ article.url }}\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\">\n    <div class=\"article__date\">{{ article.date }}\n      <span class=\"article__tag article__tag--{{ article.tag }}\">{{ article.tagLabel }}</span>\n    </div>\n    <h2 class=\"article__title\">{{ article.title }}</h2>\n    <div class=\"article__source\">{{ article.source }}</div>\n    <p class=\"article__desc\">{{ article.desc }}</p>\n  </a>\n{% endfor %}\n```\n\n### 5. SEO files — Generated from data\n- **sitemap.njk**: Loops over 11ty's `collections.all` to auto-list all pages\n- **robots.njk**: Simple template referencing `site.url`\n- **llms.njk**: Pulls org info from `site.json`, board members, etc.\n\n### 6. `Dockerfile` — Multi-stage build\n```dockerfile\nFROM node:20-alpine AS build\nWORKDIR /app\nCOPY package*.json ./\nRUN npm ci --production\nCOPY . .\nRUN npx @11ty/eleventy\n\nFROM nginx:alpine\nRUN rm /etc/nginx/conf.d/default.conf\nCOPY nginx.conf /etc/nginx/conf.d/bosarsa.conf\nCOPY --from=build /app/_site/ /usr/share/nginx/html/\nEXPOSE 80\n```\n\n### 7. `.eleventy.js` — Config\n- Input: `src/`, Output: `_site/`\n- Passthrough copy: `fonts/`, `favicon/`, `*.mp4`, `*.png`, `*.webp`, `nginx.conf`\n- Nunjucks as template engine\n\n## Also update impressum & datenschutz\n\nBoth currently have the old simple nav/footer. They'll get the full nav and full footer from the shared includes — consistent with presse.html and index.html.\n\n## What stays the same\n\n- `nginx.conf` — identical\n- `docker-compose.yml` — identical\n- `deploy-production.sh` — identical\n- All static assets (fonts, favicons, videos, images)\n- The final HTML output looks exactly the same to visitors\n\n## Implementation Steps\n\n1. `npm init` + install `@11ty/eleventy`\n2. Create `.eleventy.js` config\n3. Create `src/_data/site.json` and `src/_data/presse.json`\n4. Create `src/css/base.css` with all shared styles\n5. Create `src/_includes/base.njk`, `nav.njk`, `footer.njk`\n6. Convert `index.html` -> `src/index.njk`\n7. Convert `presse.html` -> `src/presse.njk` (data-driven loop)\n8. Convert `impressum.html` -> `src/impressum.njk`\n9. Convert `datenschutz.html` -> `src/datenschutz.njk`\n10. Create `src/robots.njk`, `src/sitemap.njk`, `src/llms.njk`\n11. Update `Dockerfile` to multi-stage build\n12. Update `.gitignore` (add `_site/`, `node_modules/`)\n13. Delete old root HTML files\n14. Test build locally, verify output matches current site\n15. Deploy\n\n## Verification\n\n1. Run `npx @11ty/eleventy` — should produce `_site/` with all files\n2. Compare `_site/index.html` output with current `index.html`\n3. Verify `_site/presse.html` renders all 6 articles\n4. Verify `_site/sitemap.xml`, `_site/robots.txt`, `_site/llms.txt` content\n5. Deploy and verify live site at https://bosarsa.oeradio.at",
  "new_string": "# Plan: Neue Sektion \"Krisenkommunikation\" auf der Startseite\n\n## Context\n\nBOS-ARSA führt zwei regelmäßige Funkübungen durch, die auf der Website dargestellt werden sollen:\n\n1. **BOS-ARSA Krisenkommunikationsübung** — Jeden Sonntag ab 18:45 Lokalzeit. Start am OE8XNK (Gerlitzen) auf 145.7625 MHz, -0.6 MHz Offset. Abfrage auf Kärntner Repeatern, dann auf Direktfrequenz. Österreichweit über den OE-Link-Verbund.\n\n2. **Notfunk Runde Österreich** — Jeden 1. Mittwoch im Monat, 17:15 UTC auf 3643 kHz ± QRM. Monatliche Notfunk-Runde für ganz Österreich.\n\n## Platzierung\n\nNeue Sektion **zwischen \"Ziele\" (#ziele) und \"Mitglied werden\" (#mitglied)** einfügen. Logischer Fluss: \"Wofür wir stehen\" → \"Was wir regelmäßig tun\" → \"Werde Teil davon\".\n\n- Section-ID: `#funkbetrieb`\n- Stil: `section--alt` (heller Hintergrund, leicht abgesetzt) — keine weitere Dark-Section, da Ziele bereits dunkel ist\n- Nav-Link: \"Funkbetrieb\" hinzufügen zwischen \"Ziele\" und \"Mitglied werden\"\n\n## Design\n\nZwei Karten nebeneinander (responsive: untereinander auf Mobilgeräten):\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────┐  ┌─────────────────────────────┐\n│  📻  Krisenkommunikations-  │  │  🌐  Notfunk Runde          │\n│      übung                  │  │      Österreich              │\n│                             │  │                              │\n│  Jeden Sonntag              │  │  Jeden 1. Mittwoch/Monat     │\n│  ab 18:45 Lokalzeit         │  │  17:15h UTC                  │\n│                             │  │                              │\n│  OE8XNK 145.7625 MHz       │  │  3643 kHz ± QRM              │\n│  -0.6 MHz Offset            │  │                              │\n│                             │  │  Monatliche Notfunk-Runde    │\n│  Wöchentliche Übungsrunde   │  │  für ganz Österreich.        │\n│  ab Gerlitzen 2m, weiter    │  │                              │\n│  auf Kärntner Repeatern,    │  │                              │\n│  Direktfrequenz und         │  │                              │\n│  österreichweit via         │  │                              │\n│  OE-Link-Verbund.           │  │                              │\n└─────────────────────────────┘  └─────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\nKarten-Stil: Ähnlich wie `.feature-card`, aber mit Frequenz-Info prominent dargestellt. Frequenz in Monospace-Font, gold-hervorgehoben.\n\n## Dateien\n\n### 1. `src/index.njk` — Neue Sektion einfügen\nZwischen `</section><!-- Ziele -->` und `<section class=\"section join\" id=\"mitglied\">`:\n- Section-Header mit Label \"Funkbetrieb\", Titel \"Unsere Übungsrunden\"\n- Zwei Karten im 2-Spalten-Grid\n- Lucide-Icons: `radio-tower` für BOS-ARSA, `globe` für Notfunk Runde\n\n### 2. `src/css/index.css` — Neue Styles\n- `.schedule-grid` — 2-Spalten-Grid (responsive)\n- `.schedule-card` — Karten-Stil basierend auf `.feature-card`\n- `.schedule-card__freq` — Frequenz-Anzeige, Monospace, Gold\n\n### 3. `src/_includes/nav.njk` — Nav-Link hinzufügen\n\"Funkbetrieb\" Link zwischen \"Ziele\" und \"Mitglied werden\"\n\n## Verifikation\n\n1. `npx @11ty/eleventy` — Build erfolgreich\n2. Sektion sichtbar zwischen Ziele und Mitglied werden\n3. Responsive: 2 Spalten Desktop → 1 Spalte Mobil\n4. Nav-Link scrollt korrekt zum Abschnitt\n5. Deploy und live verifizieren"
}
```
